Index health is judged per request with no circuit breaker (`ASM-BE-073`). No state is remembered between requests.\n\nThe one lifecycle is that of a single request:\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| RECEIVED | REJECTED | validation fails | any rule in `requirements` FR-003, 006, 009, 012, 016 | \n| RECEIVED | QUERYING | validation passes | authentication, if any, runs before validation. It is not specified (`Q-BE-003`). |\n| QUERYING | RESPONDED | index query returns | including an empty result or a page beyond the end |\n| QUERYING | UNAVAILABLE | index query errors or exceeds the request deadline | `ASM-BE-073`; the deadline value is unstated (`GAP-BE-070`) |\n| QUERYING | FAILED | any other unhandled error | |\n\n**Terminal states** (the request ends): REJECTED (400 `VALIDATION_ERROR`), RESPONDED (200), UNAVAILABLE (503 `SEARCH_UNAVAILABLE`, `Retry-After` header) and FAILED (500 `INTERNAL_ERROR`). The status and registry codes are those of the project `backend.error-model`, whose code names are proposals (`ASM-BE-001`). Every state is terminal for its request, and no state is re-entered.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Requirements\n\nAll requirements implement `API-SEARCH-580505`. Query parameter names (`q`, `tags`, `page`, `limit`, `include_archived`) come from the stories. Their types and constraints are carried by the rendered contract, not restated here.\n\n### Keyword\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-001 Keyword match.**\nGiven notes whose title or body contain the keyword, when `GET /notes/search?q=keyword` is called, then the response is 200 with the matching notes, pinned first, then by relevance.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-01 (AC1), BR-019, US-006-03 (AC1)\n- **Verification.** `search.keyword.matches_title_and_body` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n- Match semantics: `ASM-BE-067`. The relevance function is unspecified (`GAP-BE-072`).\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-002 Keyword with no match.**\nGiven no note matches the keyword, when the search runs, then the response is 200 with an empty result set, not an error.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-01 (AC2), US-006-03 (AC3)\n- **Verification.** `search.keyword.no_match_returns_empty_200` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-003 No usable filter.**\nGiven `q` is missing, empty or whitespace-only, and no `tags` value is supplied, when the search runs, then the response is 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` and the index is not queried.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-01 (AC3), `CON-BE-003`, `ASM-BE-015`, `ASM-BE-076`\n- **Verification.** `search.validation.no_q_and_no_tags_is_400` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n\n### Tags\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-004 Tag filter.**\nGiven notes tagged `project-x` exist, when `GET /notes/search?tags=project-x` is called, then only notes carrying that tag are returned.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-02 (AC1), US-006-03 (AC2)\n- **Verification.** `search.tags.single_tag_filters` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n- A tags-only search is allowed (`ASM-BE-015`).\n- Several tags use the `tags` format and the all-of rule of `ASM-BE-070` and `ASM-BE-071` (`Q-BE-055`).\n- Tag names are compared exactly as stored (`ASM-BE-077`).\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-005 Tag with no match.**\nGiven no note carries the tag, when the search runs, then the response is 200 with an empty result set.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-02 (AC2)\n- **Verification.** `search.tags.no_match_returns_empty_200` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-006 Invalid tag characters.**\nGiven the `tags` parameter contains invalid characters, when the search is made, then the response is 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` with error detail.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-02 (AC3)\n- **Verification.** `search.tags.invalid_characters_is_400` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n- The valid character set is not defined by any input (`GAP-BE-010`).\n\n### Combination\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-007 Combined filters.**\nGiven `q=budget` and `tags=finance`, when the search runs, then only notes matching both are returned.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-04 (AC1), BR-010\n- **Verification.** `search.combined.keyword_and_tag_intersect` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-008 Combination that excludes everything.**\nGiven the keyword matches but the tag filter excludes all matching notes, when the search runs, then the response is 200 with an empty result set.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-04 (AC2)\n- **Verification.** `search.combined.tag_excludes_all_returns_empty_200` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-009 Malformed combination.**\nGiven a malformed filter combination, when the search is made, then the response is 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR`. A request is malformed if either of these holds (`ASM-BE-079`):\n- a parameter is supplied more than once;\n- a parameter is not one of the five named above.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-04 (AC3)\n- **Verification.** `search.validation.malformed_combination_is_400` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n\n### Pagination\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-010 Pages and total.**\nGiven more matching notes than the page size, when `page` and `limit` are supplied, then the requested subset is returned together with the total count of all matches.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-05 (AC1), BR-011\n- **Verification.** `search.pagination.subset_and_total` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-011 Page beyond the end.**\nGiven a `page` beyond the available results, when the search runs, then the response is 200 with an empty result set.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-05 (AC2)\n- **Verification.** `search.pagination.page_beyond_end_returns_empty_200` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-012 Invalid paging values.**\nGiven a negative or non-numeric `page` or `limit`, when the search is made, then the response is 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR`. Under `ASM-BE-068` this also covers `page` = 0 and `limit` = 0.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-05 (AC3)\n- **Verification.** `search.pagination.invalid_values_is_400` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-013 Bounded result set.**\nGiven no `page` or `limit`, when the search runs, then a default page and page size apply. A `limit` above the maximum is rejected with 400 (`Q-BE-054`). A result set is never unbounded.\n- **Traces to.** BR-011, NFR-007, `ASM-BE-009`, `ASM-BE-068`\n- **Verification.** `search.pagination.defaults_and_max_enforced` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n\n### Archived and pinned\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-014 Archived excluded by default.**\nGiven both archived and active notes match, when a default search runs, then only active notes are returned.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-06 (AC1), BR-009\n- **Verification.** `search.archived.excluded_by_default` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-015 Archived on request.**\nGiven `include_archived=true`, when the search runs, then archived notes are included alongside active notes.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-06 (AC2), BR-009\n- **Verification.** `search.archived.included_when_requested` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n- The operation record says archived notes are excluded and does not mention this parameter (`CON-BE-054`).\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-016 Invalid `include_archived`.**\nGiven an `include_archived` value other than `true` or `false`, when the search is made, then the response is 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR`.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-06 (AC3), `ASM-BE-069`\n- **Verification.** `search.archived.invalid_value_is_400` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-017 Pinned first across the whole result set.**\nGiven pinned and unpinned notes match, when the search runs, then every pinned match precedes every unpinned match across all pages. The ordering is applied before pagination, not within each page.\n- **Traces to.** BR-019, US-003-01 (AC1)\n- **Verification.** `search.ordering.pinned_before_unpinned_across_pages` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-018 Stable order within a group.**\nGiven several matches in the same pinned group, when pages are requested in sequence, then the order is the same on every request. It is relevance (descending) when `q` is present, otherwise latest update first, with the note id as the final tie-break (`ASM-BE-072`). No note appears on two pages and none is skipped between pages.\n- **Traces to.** BR-019 (\"ordering \u2026 unchanged\"), US-003-05\n- **Verification.** `search.ordering.deterministic_across_pages` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n- The prior ordering is not defined by any input (`ASM-BE-072`).\n\n### Non-functional\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-019 Latency.**\nGiven 50 requests per second, when searches run against about 100k notes, then p95 latency is under 300 ms. This holds for page sizes up to 100.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-006, NFR-007, US-003-03 (AC1, AC2)\n- **Verification.** `search.perf.p95_under_300ms_at_50rps_100k_notes` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n- The environment is not defined (`GAP-BE-073`).\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-020 Degraded index.**\nGiven the search index is degraded or unavailable, when a search is requested, then the response is 503 `SEARCH_UNAVAILABLE` with a `Retry-After` header. The request does not hang and no partial results are returned.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-03 (AC3)\n- **Verification.** `search.availability.degraded_index_returns_503_retry_after` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n- The `Retry-After` value and the request deadline are unstated (`GAP-BE-070`).\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-021 Input is data.**\nGiven a keyword or tag containing SQL metacharacters or search-syntax operators, when the search runs, then the value is treated as plain data. It causes no query error and no injection.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-012\n- **Verification.** `search.security.injection_inputs_treated_as_data` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-022 Route precedence.**\nGiven `GET /notes/search` and `GET /notes/{id}` both exist, when `/notes/search` is called, then the search handler answers it. It is never read as a note id.\n- **Traces to.** `backend.api-surface`: both operations are in the project api-surface. `platform.md` (Express) is the router.\n- **Verification.** `search.routing.search_path_not_captured_by_get_note` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`\n\n### Story coverage owned by this module\n\n| Story | Requirements |\n|---|---|\n| US-003-01 | FR-001, 002, 003 |\n| US-003-02 | FR-004, 005, 006 |\n| US-003-03 | FR-019, 020 |\n| US-003-04 | FR-007, 008, 009 |\n| US-003-05 | FR-010, 011, 012, 013 |\n| US-003-06 | FR-014, 015, 016 |\n| US-006-03 | FR-001, 002, 004, 005. These cover the backend half. The search box UI is the web_app's. |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-SEARCH-580505` | GET | `/notes/search` | `search` | 200 | 400, 500, 503 | Search active notes by keyword, tag or both, with pagination. Archived notes are excluded and pinned notes are ordered first. Returns 503 with Retry-After when the search index is degraded. Path is a proposal; no input names it. |\n\n### `API-SEARCH-580505` \u2014 GET `/notes/search`\n\nSearch active notes by keyword, tag or both, with pagination. Archived notes are excluded and pinned notes are ordered first. Returns 503 with Retry-After when the search index is degraded. Path is a proposal; no input names it.\n\n**Request:** \n- `q` (string): Keyword matched against note title and body. At least one of q or tags is required (ASM-BE-015); a missing or empty q with no tags is a 400.\n- `tags` (array): Tag names to filter by, combinable with q (combined filter). Sent as a repeated or comma-separated query parameter.\n- `page` (integer): 1-based page number. Invalid or out-of-range values return 400.\n- `page_size` (integer): Results per page; default 20, maximum 100 (ASM-BE-009). Values over the maximum or below 1 return 400.\n**Response:** \n- `items` (array, required): Matching non-archived notes for the requested page, pinned notes first. Each item has id, title, body, pinned, tags, status, created_at, updated_at.\n- `page` (integer, required): Current page number.\n- `page_size` (integer, required): Page size applied to this response.\n- `total` (integer, required): Total number of matching non-archived notes across all pages.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.invariants | status: DERIVED -->\n## Invariants\n\n**INV-BE-051.** Every returned note satisfies every filter in the request:\n- it carries the keyword match, if `q` was supplied;\n- it carries all supplied tags, if `tags` was supplied;\n- it is active, unless `include_archived=true`.\n(US-003-01, 02, 04, 06; BR-009, BR-010)\n\n**INV-BE-052.** In the full ordered result set, no unpinned note precedes a pinned note, on any page. (BR-019)\n\n**INV-BE-053.** Search changes no data. A search leaves `notes` and `note_tags` byte-identical, and the same request may be repeated freely. (derived: no write path, `data-model`)\n\n**INV-BE-054.** For a fixed data set, the pages of one query are disjoint and together form the full ordered result set. The reported total equals the size of that set. (US-003-05; FR-018)\n\n**INV-BE-055.** A 503 response carries no result items. A 400 response means no index query ran. (US-003-03 AC3; FR-003, FR-020)\n\n**INV-BE-056.** A result is never unbounded: the number of returned items never exceeds the maximum page size. (BR-011, `ASM-BE-009`)\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.authorization | status: BLOCKED -->\n## Authorization\n\n**BLOCKED.** The operation \u00d7 role matrix and the ownership predicate cannot be written without inventing an authorisation rule. The project `backend.auth-model` is itself BLOCKED, for the same reasons.\n\n| Missing decision | What it decides here | What would unblock it |\n|---|---|---|\n| `Q-BE-003`: what a successful verify issues, and whether note and search endpoints require it | whether `API-SEARCH-580505` needs a credential, and whether it documents 401 or 403 (US-004-03 expects them for authenticated endpoints) | the post-verify credential decision |\n| `Q-BE-002`: whether notes are owned per user or shared by the team workspace (BR-031) | whether results are restricted to a caller's own notes. This is the ownership predicate and it would sit in the query's filter. | the ownership decision and the access rule per operation |\n\nOnce unblocked, the section will hold the role matrix for this one read operation and the ownership predicate applied to the result set. Until then no role, no credential requirement and no ownership scoping is assumed.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Jobs\n\n**Scheduled work: none.** The indexes live in the notes database and are updated with each note change (`ASM-BE-074`, `ASM-BE-010`). No sweep, reindex or backfill job is defined by any input, so none is specified. If the index were external or asynchronous, that assumption would need to change.\n\n**Retries: none on the server.** A degraded index returns 503 with `Retry-After`, and the client decides whether to retry (`ASM-BE-073`). There is no server-side retry and no circuit breaker.\n\n**Idempotency.** `GET` is safe and idempotent (`INV-BE-053`). No idempotency key applies.\n\nRetention deletion for login data is owned by `auth` (NFR-019) and is not a search concern.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.test-plan | status: DERIVED -->\n## Test plan\n\nTest names are planned. Requirement ids are the `BE-SEARCH-FR-` ids above.\n\n**Unit**\n- Parameter parsing and validation: `search.validation.*`, `search.tags.invalid_characters_is_400`, `search.pagination.invalid_values_is_400`, `search.archived.invalid_value_is_400` (FR-003, 006, 009, 012, 016).\n- Keyword tokenisation and case handling: `search.keyword.words_all_must_match_case_insensitive` (FR-001, `ASM-BE-067`).\n- Ordering comparator for pinned, relevance, update time and id tie-break: `search.ordering.*` (FR-017, 018).\n\n**Integration** (PostgreSQL 16, seeded notes and tags)\n- Filters: `search.keyword.*`, `search.tags.*`, `search.combined.*`, `search.archived.*` (FR-001 to 008, 014 to 016).\n- Pagination: `search.pagination.*` (FR-010 to 013).\n- Ordering across pages: `search.ordering.pinned_before_unpinned_across_pages` and `search.ordering.deterministic_across_pages` (FR-017, 018, INV-BE-052, 054).\n- Read-your-writes: `search.consistency.new_edited_archived_note_visible_to_next_search` (`ASM-BE-074`).\n- Degraded index: `search.availability.degraded_index_returns_503_retry_after`, using a forced index failure (FR-020).\n- Injection inputs: `search.security.injection_inputs_treated_as_data` (FR-021).\n- Routing: `search.routing.search_path_not_captured_by_get_note` (FR-022).\n\n**Contract**\n- `GET /notes/search` is present in the published spec with its 400 and 503 responses and its example payloads for search (BR-012, NFR-008, NFR-009, US-004-03, US-004-05).\n- The contract documents `include_archived` (`CON-BE-054`).\n- The contract-docs coverage check reports this operation as covered (US-004-04).\n- The scope-guard contract suite finds no overlap between `/notes/search` and an excluded category (NFR-010).\n\n**Non-functional**\n- `search.perf.p95_under_300ms_at_50rps_100k_notes` (FR-019), and again at page size 100 (NFR-007). The environment is `GAP-BE-073`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.gaps | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Gaps\n\n### Contradiction checks run\n\n- **Story \u2194 design.** `design-brief.md` carries acceptance criteria only (AC-001-01-01 to AC-002-01-03), none about search. Nothing found.\n- **Story \u2194 requirement.** `CON-BE-003` (cited, defined in the project spec) concerns a missing `q`. `CON-BE-054` is raised here.\n- **Requirement \u2194 requirement.** BR-019 says ordering among notes with equal pinned value is \"unchanged\", but no prior ordering is defined (`ASM-BE-072`). BR-011 and NFR-007 agree. No other conflict found.\n- **Design \u2194 design.** Not applicable.\n\n### Contradictions\n\n**CON-BE-054 (non-critical).** The operation record for `API-SEARCH-580505` says archived notes are excluded and gives no override. BR-009 and US-003-06 (AC2) say the default excludes archived notes and a caller may pass `include_archived=true`.\n- Reading used: BR-009 and US-003-06, the explicit requirements (FR-014, 015, 016).\n- If the record wins: the override is not offered, US-003-06 AC2 fails, and no client can reach archived notes through search.\n- If BR-009 wins: the record text is incomplete and the rendered contract must carry `include_archived`.\n- Affected sections: `requirements` (FR-014 to 016).\n\n### Questions\n\nAll three are `degradable`. This run is unattended, so each takes its stated default.\n\n| ID | Question | Class | Default taken |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `Q-BE-054` | Is a `limit` above the maximum rejected with 400 or clamped to the maximum? | degradable | rejected with 400 (`ASM-BE-068`) |\n| `Q-BE-055` | With several tags, must a note carry all of them or any of them? | degradable | all of them (`ASM-BE-071`) |\n| `Q-BE-056` | Is a whitespace-only `q` treated as empty? | degradable | treated as empty, so 400 when no tags (`ASM-BE-076`) |\n\n### Assumptions\n\n- **ASM-BE-067.** `q` is split into words. A note matches when every word appears in its title or body, case-insensitively. The `simple` text-search configuration is used: no stemming, no synonyms. No input names a language configuration. Confirm with the product owner. Used by FR-001 and `data-model`.\n- **ASM-BE-068.** `page` is 1-based with default 1, and the default and maximum `limit` are those of `ASM-BE-009`. `page` below 1, `limit` below 1 or above the maximum, and non-integers are rejected with 400 (`Q-BE-054`).\n- **ASM-BE-069.** `include_archived` defaults to false and accepts only `true` and `false`.\n- **ASM-BE-070.** `tags` is one comma-separated parameter. An empty element, as in `a,,b`, is rejected with 400.\n- **ASM-BE-071.** Multiple tags are all-of: a note must carry every supplied tag (`Q-BE-055`). US-003-02 says \"one or more tags\" and does not say which. US-003-04 says \"matching both\" for keyword and tag.\n- **ASM-BE-072.** Within a pinned group, order is relevance (descending) when `q` is present. Otherwise it is the note's updated timestamp (descending). Ties are broken by note id (ascending). This definition is mine; BR-019 gives none.\n- **ASM-BE-073.** An index query error, or exceeding a request deadline, yields 503. It is judged per request, with no circuit breaker and no stored index-health state.\n- **ASM-BE-074.** The index is maintained in the notes database with each note change, so the next search sees the change. No asynchronous lag exists.\n- **ASM-BE-075.** Each result item uses the same note representation as the get-note response (`notes` owns it), plus the pagination envelope carried by the rendered contract.\n- **ASM-BE-076.** A whitespace-only `q` is treated as empty (`Q-BE-056`).\n- **ASM-BE-077.** Tag names are compared exactly as stored: case-sensitive, no normalisation (`GAP-BE-010`).\n- **ASM-BE-078.** The ordering index `idx_notes_order` is a performance proposal for tags-only searches. It may be dropped if load tests show it is unnecessary.\n- **ASM-BE-079.** A malformed filter combination is a repeated parameter, or a parameter outside `q`, `tags`, `page`, `limit` and `include_archived`. The latter is an assumption, not stated by any input. It means a misspelt parameter fails loudly. Confirm that web_app does not send extra parameters, such as cache-busters.\n\n### Gaps\n\n**GAP-BE-070.** The `Retry-After` value and format on 503, and the request deadline after which a search counts as hanging, are unstated.\n- Degrades: FR-020, `state-machine` (UNAVAILABLE).\n- Instead: the header is required, and the value is left to the error-handling requirement.\n- Closing artefact: an error-handling requirement. This refines `GAP-BE-011`.\n\n**GAP-BE-071.** Not stated: minimum or maximum length of `q`, maximum number of tags in one request, maximum page depth.\n- Degrades: FR-003, 006, 013, FR-019.\n- Instead: no bound is imposed beyond the page size.\n- Closing artefact: validation requirements.\n\n**GAP-BE-072.** The relevance ranking function is unspecified. US-003-01 says only \"ordered by relevance\".\n- Degrades: FR-001, FR-018.\n- Instead: relevance means the ranking of the index match, and ties are broken by `ASM-BE-072`.\n- Closing artefact: a ranking requirement, or a decision that the index default is accepted.\n\n**GAP-BE-073.** The environment for verifying NFR-006 and NFR-007 is unstated: hardware, connection pool, data distribution and tag cardinality.\n- Degrades: FR-019, and the `test-plan` performance test.\n- Instead: the test is planned against 50 requests per second and about 100k notes only.\n- Closing artefact: a performance-test environment definition.\n\n### Gaps defined in the project spec and relevant here\n\n- `GAP-BE-001`: the contents of the four inbound handoffs (`HANDOFF-WEBAPP-263975`, `-452996`, `-500592`, `-732181`) were not supplied. This module cannot say which of them it answers.\n- `GAP-BE-005`: no operation lists active notes or archived notes only. `include_archived=true` mixes both, so it is not an archived-only view (US-006-05). Plainly stated, the web_app needs an archived-only list or filter and this module does not provide one.\n- `GAP-BE-006`: encryption at rest is not stated.\n- `GAP-BE-010`: tag rules are not stated.\n- `GAP-BE-011`: the `Retry-After` value on 503 is not stated; `GAP-BE-070` narrows it.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-003-01", "US-003-02", "US-003-03", "US-003-04", "US-003-05", "US-003-06", "US-006-03"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/auth-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/auth-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.frontmatter | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Frontmatter\n\n```yaml\nid: auth\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [Q-BE-001, Q-BE-003]\nscope: [API-AUTH-015365, API-AUTH-592570]\n```\n\n`blocked_by` lists the two blocking questions that stop parts of this module. `Q-BE-001` is who may request, verify or receive a code (BR-030). `Q-BE-003` is what a successful verify issues. `Q-BE-002` (note ownership) does not block this module.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.purpose | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** `auth` owns email one-time-code login (US-006-06; BR-020 to BR-033; NFR-015 to NFR-019). It:\n\n- issues a 6-digit numeric code for an email address (BR-029) through `API-AUTH-015365`, and sends it by email;\n- limits requests to 3 per rolling 10 minutes per email (BR-021, BR-028);\n- verifies a submitted code through `API-AUTH-592570`, with these rules:\n  - a code expires 10 minutes after issue (BR-020, BR-024, BR-026);\n  - a code is single use (BR-023);\n  - 5 wrong codes lock verification for 15 minutes (BR-022, BR-025, BR-027);\n- answers each refusal with the status the requirements give (BR-033);\n- keeps and deletes its three record kinds (NFR-019).\n\n**Not responsible for:**\n\n- What a successful verify issues: credential type, lifetime, transport and refresh (`Q-BE-003`). Until it is decided, this module specifies that a code was accepted and nothing about what follows.\n- Deciding who may request or receive a code (`Q-BE-001`, BR-030).\n- Whether `notes` or `search` require a credential, and the note-ownership predicate (`Q-BE-002`, `Q-BE-003`; `backend.auth-model` is BLOCKED).\n- The global error envelope and the registry. They are defined in `backend.error-model`, and this module only uses its codes.\n- Publishing the OpenAPI document and the data-residency note (`contract-docs`).\n- The login and code-entry screens and their error copy (web_app).\n- Operating the transactional email service. This module only calls it (NFR-017).\n- Existing login methods, which NFR-016 says \"remain unchanged\". No input describes them (`GAP-BE-007`).\n- Four web_app handoffs (`HANDOFF-WEBAPP-263975`, `HANDOFF-WEBAPP-452996`, `HANDOFF-WEBAPP-500592`, `HANDOFF-WEBAPP-732181`) were listed for this run but their contents were not supplied. They are therefore not satisfied here (`GAP-BE-001`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Data model\n\nThree tables, named by NFR-019 (\"code challenges, send attempts and account locks\"). They correspond to migrations 004 to 006 in `backend.migration-plan`, and the store is PostgreSQL 16 (`platform.md`).\n\nConventions are in `ASM-BE-117`:\n\n- Surrogate keys are `bigint` identity columns and are never exposed.\n- Every timestamp is `timestamptz`, stored and compared in UTC.\n- Every `email` value is stored trimmed and lower-cased.\n- All three tables are keyed by email address, because no account entity exists (project `ASM-BE-002`).\n\n### `login_code_challenges`\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | bigint identity | NOT NULL | generated | PRIMARY KEY |\n| `email` | text | NOT NULL | none | **PII** |\n| `code` | char(6) | NOT NULL | none | CHECK `code ~ '^[0-9]{6}$'` (BR-029); **credential-like** |\n| `issued_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | database `now()` | none |\n| `used` | boolean | NOT NULL | `false` | none |\n\n- **Expiry is derived, not stored.** A challenge is expired when `now() >= issued_at + 10 minutes` (`ASM-BE-004` in the project spec).\n- **Foreign keys.** None, so no `ON DELETE` behaviour applies.\n- **At-rest treatment.** Encryption, hashing or redaction of `email` and `code` is not stated by any input, so no claim is made (`GAP-BE-006`).\n\n| Index | Serves |\n|---|---|\n| `(email, code, issued_at DESC)` | Verify lookup: the most recent challenge for this email with this code (`ASM-BE-120`). |\n| `(issued_at)` | Retention sweep: delete where `issued_at` is older than the cutoff. |\n\n### `login_send_attempts`\n\nOne row per **accepted** request-code call. Refused calls write no row (project `ASM-BE-005`).\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | bigint identity | NOT NULL | generated | PRIMARY KEY |\n| `email` | text | NOT NULL | none | **PII** |\n| `requested_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | database `now()` | none |\n\n| Index | Serves |\n|---|---|\n| `(email, requested_at DESC)` | Resend limit: count rows for an email with `requested_at > now() - 10 minutes`. |\n| `(requested_at)` | Retention sweep. |\n\n### `account_locks`\n\nOne row per email that has had at least one counted wrong code (`ASM-BE-119`).\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `email` | text | NOT NULL | none | PRIMARY KEY; **PII** |\n| `failed_count` | smallint | NOT NULL | `0` | CHECK `failed_count BETWEEN 0 AND 5` |\n| `locked_until` | timestamptz | NULL | `NULL` | CHECK `locked_until IS NULL OR failed_count = 5` |\n| `updated_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | database `now()` | Retention clock |\n\n| Index | Serves |\n|---|---|\n| PRIMARY KEY `(email)` | Lock check and counter update, per email. |\n| `(updated_at)` | Retention sweep. |\n\n**Time source.** Every comparison uses the database clock (`ASM-BE-118`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## State machine\n\nThree independent state sets exist.\n\n### Code challenge\n\n```mermaid\nstateDiagram-v2\n    [*] --> ISSUED: accepted request-code\n    ISSUED --> USED: verify matches, not expired, not used\n    ISSUED --> EXPIRED: 10 minutes elapse (derived, not stored)\n    USED --> DELETED: retention sweep\n    EXPIRED --> DELETED: retention sweep\n    ISSUED --> DELETED: retention sweep\n```\n\n- **Terminal states:** `USED`, `EXPIRED`, `DELETED`. `USED` is stored (`used = true`); `EXPIRED` is computed.\n- A challenge that is `USED` and also past 10 minutes stays `USED`. Precedence is used before expired (project `ASM-BE-007`).\n- After `DELETED`, the same code is treated as unknown. Verifying it returns invalid (401) and counts as a wrong code (see `GAP-BE-123`).\n\n| Transition | Trigger | Guard |\n|---|---|---|\n| (none) \u2192 `ISSUED` | `API-AUTH-015365` accepted | Fewer than 3 accepted attempts for the email in the rolling 10 minutes; input valid; email delivered (`ASM-BE-122`). |\n| `ISSUED` \u2192 `USED` | `API-AUTH-592570` | Not locked; most recent challenge with equal code; `used = false`; `now() < issued_at + 10 minutes`. Done as one atomic compare-and-set. |\n| `ISSUED` \u2192 `EXPIRED` | time | `now() >= issued_at + 10 minutes`. |\n| any \u2192 `DELETED` | retention sweep | Older than the retention period (`backend.auth.jobs`). |\n\n### Account verification state, per email\n\n| State | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `OPEN(n)`, n = 0..4 | `failed_count = n` and no active lock. |\n| `LOCKED` | `failed_count = 5` and `now() < locked_until`. |\n\n| Transition | Trigger | Guard / effect |\n|---|---|---|\n| `OPEN(n)` \u2192 `OPEN(n+1)` | verify outcome invalid (401) | n+1 < 5. Increment atomically. |\n| `OPEN(4)` \u2192 `LOCKED` | the 5th invalid verify | That request still returns 401. `locked_until = now() + 15 minutes` (`ASM-BE-121`). |\n| `OPEN(n)` \u2192 `OPEN(0)` | successful verify | Counter reset (project `ASM-BE-005`). |\n| `LOCKED` \u2192 `OPEN(0)` | `now() >= locked_until` observed | Reset `failed_count` to 0 and clear `locked_until`. |\n| `LOCKED` \u2192 `LOCKED` | any verify | Refused with 423. No counter change, and the lock is not extended. |\n\n- **Terminal states:** none. The account state is cyclic, and the row is removed only by retention.\n- **Request-code never changes this state.** It is not refused during a lock (BR-032).\n\n### Resend window (derived)\n\nThe state is `ACCEPTING` while the count of attempts with `requested_at > now() - 10 minutes` is below 3. Otherwise it is `RATE_LIMITED`, and a request is refused with 429 `OTP_RATE_LIMITED`. Nothing is stored for this state.\n\n### Illegal transitions\n\nIllegal transitions are the refusals below. For the exact status and code names, see `backend.error-model`.\n\n| Attempted | Status | Code |\n|---|---|---|\n| Verify while `LOCKED` | 423 | `OTP_ACCOUNT_LOCKED` |\n| Verify a `USED` challenge | 409 | `OTP_CODE_ALREADY_USED` |\n| Verify an `EXPIRED` challenge | 410 | `OTP_CODE_EXPIRED` |\n| Verify a code that matches nothing | 401 | `OTP_CODE_INVALID` |\n| Request while `RATE_LIMITED` | 429 | `OTP_RATE_LIMITED` |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.requirements | status: BLOCKED -->\n## Requirements\n\n**BLOCKED.** the run did not produce this section for module `auth`. Nothing has been written in its place.\n\nThis section is required by the surface's template and was not produced. It is emitted blocked rather than omitted: a missing section is invisible, a blocked one is a work item.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-AUTH-015365` | POST | `/auth/request-code` | `auth` | 202 | 400, 429, 500 | Request a 6-digit one-time login code, emailed to the supplied address. Limited to 3 requests per rolling 10 minutes per email; not refused during an account lock (BR-032). |\n| `API-AUTH-592570` | POST | `/auth/verify-code` | `auth` | 200 | 400, 401, 409, 410, 423, 500 | Verify a one-time login code. Codes expire 10 minutes after issue and are single use. Five wrong codes lock verification for 15 minutes. Precedence: lock, then used, then expired, then invalid. |\n\n### `API-AUTH-015365` \u2014 POST `/auth/request-code`\n\nRequest a 6-digit one-time login code, emailed to the supplied address. Limited to 3 requests per rolling 10 minutes per email; not refused during an account lock (BR-032).\n\n**Request:** \n- `email` (string, required): Email address to which the one-time login code is sent. PII; always masked in examples and logs.\n**Response:** \n- `message` (string): Human-readable confirmation that a code was issued and emailed. Never contains the code or an unmasked email address.\n\n### `API-AUTH-592570` \u2014 POST `/auth/verify-code`\n\nVerify a one-time login code. Codes expire 10 minutes after issue and are single use. Five wrong codes lock verification for 15 minutes. Precedence: lock, then used, then expired, then invalid.\n\n**Request:** \n- `email` (string, required): Email address the code was issued to. PII; always masked in examples and logs.\n- `code` (string, required): The 6-digit numeric one-time code received by email.\n**Response:** \n- `verified` (boolean): True when the code was accepted and consumed. What credential a successful verify issues (type, lifetime, transport) is undecided and blocked on Q-BE-003, so no credential field is defined.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Invariants\n\n- **INV-BE-101.** A challenge transitions from `used = false` to `used = true` at most once, and never back.\n- **INV-BE-102.** For any email and any instant, the number of `login_send_attempts` rows with `requested_at` in the preceding 10 minutes is at most 3.\n- **INV-BE-103.** `failed_count` is between 0 and 5, and `locked_until` is non-null only when `failed_count = 5`.\n- **INV-BE-104.** While `now() < locked_until` for an email, no verify for it succeeds, and none changes `failed_count` or `locked_until`.\n- **INV-BE-105.** No verify succeeds at or after `issued_at + 10 minutes` of the matched challenge (`ASM-BE-004`).\n- **INV-BE-106.** Every stored `code` is exactly six numeric characters.\n- **INV-BE-107.** A refused request (400, 415, 423, 429, or any refusal other than 401) creates no challenge or send-attempt row. Only a 401 verify changes `failed_count`, and only a success resets it.\n- **INV-BE-108.** A code value appears only in the challenge row and the outgoing email. It never appears in a response, log entry, metric label or error body.\n- **INV-BE-109.** Every `email` in the three tables is stored in normalised form (`ASM-BE-117`), so one address maps to one lock row and one resend window.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.authorization | status: BLOCKED -->\n## Authorization\n\n**BLOCKED.** The role permission matrix and the eligibility predicate cannot be written without inventing an authorisation rule. It needs `Q-BE-001` (who may request, verify or receive a code) and `Q-BE-003` (what verify issues, which sets how the other modules authorise). `backend.auth-model` is BLOCKED for the same reason.\n\nWhat follows from the operations themselves (DERIVED):\n\n| Operation | Caller | Authentication required |\n|---|---|---|\n| `API-AUTH-015365` | any unauthenticated client | No. The operation exists to obtain access, so it cannot require the credential it leads to. |\n| `API-AUTH-592570` | any unauthenticated client | No, for the same reason. |\n\n- **No roles are defined for this module.** None of the supplied inputs names a role for login.\n- **Ownership predicate.** No resource is user-scoped here. Challenges, send attempts and locks are keyed by the email supplied in the request. A caller shows control of an address only by presenting a valid, unexpired, unused code issued to it.\n- **Abuse limits.** The only limits specified are per email (3 requests per 10 minutes; 5 wrong codes). No per-client or per-network limit is stated (`GAP-BE-124`).\n\n**Would unblock.** A clean BR-030 and the post-verify credential decision.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Jobs\n\n**Retention sweep** (BE-AUTH-FR-017; NFR-019; migration 008).\n\n| Table | Delete where | Period |\n|---|---|---|\n| `login_code_challenges` | `issued_at` older than period | 1 day |\n| `login_send_attempts` | `requested_at` older than period | 1 day |\n| `account_locks` | `updated_at` older than period | 5 days |\n\n- **Periods** follow `ASM-BE-011` in the project spec, because the NFR-019 units are garbled (`GAP-BE-004`).\n- **Schedule.** The run frequency is not stated (`GAP-BE-125`).\n- **Idempotency.** The sweep is a predicate delete, so a re-run or overlapping run is harmless. A failed run is retried at the next tick, and no idempotency key is needed.\n- **Effect on verify.** The sweep removes the evidence for 409 and 410. A used or expired code submitted after deletion returns 401 and counts as a wrong code (`GAP-BE-123`).\n- **No unlock job.** A lock ends by comparison with `locked_until` at request time (BE-AUTH-FR-010).\n\n**Request idempotency.**\n- No idempotency key exists in any input.\n- `API-AUTH-015365` is not idempotent: each accepted call issues a new code and uses one of the 3 allowances. The earlier unexpired codes stay valid (project `ASM-BE-008`).\n- A replay of a successful `API-AUTH-592570` returns 409.\n- The web_app should therefore avoid duplicate submits and automatic retries of the request-code call (`GAP-BE-127`).\n\n**Retries.** The module retries nothing. Email delivery retries are unstated (`GAP-BE-121`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Test plan\n\nAll time-dependent tests use a controllable clock for the boundaries in `ASM-BE-118`.\n\n**Unit**\n- `code_is_six_numeric_chars`: leading zeros kept; the format check rejects 5 or 7 digits and non-digits.\n- `verify_code_precedence_matrix`: every combination of locked, used, expired and unknown gives the status in BE-AUTH-FR-011.\n- `failed_count_only_changes_on_401_and_success`.\n- `request_code_window_rolls_at_ten_minutes`: boundaries at 9m59s and 10m00s.\n- `verify_code_expired_at_ten_minutes`: boundaries at 9m59s and 10m00s.\n- `verify_code_lock_lifts_after_fifteen_minutes`.\n- `email_normalisation_maps_case_variants_to_one_key` (`ASM-BE-117`).\n\n**Integration** (PostgreSQL 16, email service stubbed)\n- `request_code_accepts_and_emails_code` and `request_code_fourth_in_window_is_429`, including that the refused call writes no rows.\n- `request_code_allowed_while_locked`.\n- `verify_code_fifth_wrong_locks_for_fifteen_minutes` and `verify_code_correct_code_during_lock_is_423`.\n- `verify_code_second_use_is_409` and `verify_code_concurrent_only_one_wins`.\n- `concurrent_requests_never_exceed_limit` and `concurrent_wrong_codes_cap_at_five`.\n- `request_code_delivery_failure_persists_nothing`.\n- `retention_sweep_deletes_only_expired_records`, plus the post-deletion behaviour in `GAP-BE-123`.\n- `logs_contain_no_code_or_unmasked_email`: scan captured logs, metrics and responses for the issued code and the raw email.\n\n**Contract**\n- Both operations appear in the published OpenAPI document (`contract-docs`, BR-012, NFR-008).\n- Every status and registry code in the outcome table (BE-AUTH-FR-001 to FR-015) is declared on its operation. If an operation record omits one, the contract test fails (`GAP-BE-120`).\n- No response schema contains a code field outside the verify request, and no response echoes the issued code.\n- US-006-06's three acceptance criteria are each exercised by a named test above: FR-003, FR-006 and FR-009.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.gaps | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Gaps, contradictions and assumptions\n\nNo new `Q-` was minted. The blocking questions that affect this module are `Q-BE-001` and `Q-BE-003`, defined in the project spec. Project-level items relied on here are `GAP-BE-001`, `-002`, `-003`, `-004`, `-006`, `-007` and `-011`, `CON-BE-001`, and the project `ASM-BE-003`, `-004`, `-005`, `-007`, `-008`, `-011` and `-014`.\n\n### Gaps\n\n| ID | Missing | Degrades | Done instead | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-120` | The previous rendering of the two operation records listed only 400 and 415 as errors, but this module specifies 401, 409, 410, 423, 429 and 500 as well. | `backend.auth.api-contract`; the contract test | The statuses are specified here and cited from BR-033 and BR-028. | Operation records that list every status in BE-AUTH-FR-001 to FR-015. |\n| `GAP-BE-121` | Email delivery failure, bounce and retry behaviour. | BE-AUTH-FR-015 | `ASM-BE-122`: 500 and nothing persisted. | An email-delivery requirement. |\n| `GAP-BE-122` | Whether a successful verify, or a newer code, invalidates other outstanding codes for the email. | BE-AUTH-FR-007; `ASM-BE-125` | A success consumes only the matched challenge (`ASM-BE-125`; project `ASM-BE-008`). | A decision on outstanding codes. |\n| `GAP-BE-123` | No time window on the wrong-code count. After deletion of a used or expired challenge, resubmitting its code counts as wrong. | `backend.auth.state-machine`; `backend.auth.jobs` | Count is cumulative until a success or a lock ending, and is bounded in practice by the 5-day lock retention. | A statement of the counting window. |\n| `GAP-BE-124` | No per-client or per-network limit on code requests or verification attempts. Whether the response reveals if an address is eligible or known is unstated. | `backend.auth.authorization` | Only the per-email limits are specified. | Abuse-control and enumeration requirements; BR-030 (`Q-BE-001`). |\n| `GAP-BE-125` | Retention sweep frequency. | `backend.auth.jobs` | Frequency left to the implementer. | An operational requirement. |\n| `GAP-BE-126` | Email address validity rules and maximum length. | BE-AUTH-FR-014 | \"Valid email\" is taken from the operation record; no length is stated. | A validation requirement. |\n| `GAP-BE-127` | Outbound need for the web_app: the request-code call is not idempotent, so a double submit or an automatic retry uses up the 3-per-10-minutes allowance. The web_app should prevent duplicate submits and not retry that call automatically. | `backend.auth.jobs` | Stated here only. | The web_app spec addressing it. |\n\n### Contradiction\n\n| ID | Claims | Reading used and consequence |\n|---|---|---|\n| `CON-BE-104` | The `feedback.md` answer says \"Max 3 **resends** per 10 minutes\". BR-021 and BR-028 say \"resend\" but BR-032 and US-006-06 say \"more than 3 **requests** within 10 minutes\", and the `feedback.md` question asks for \"code requests per email\". | **Non-critical.** Reading used: 3 requests in total, counting the first (BR-032, US-006-06, the question text, and the operation record). If the other reading holds (1 request + 3 resends), the limit becomes 4 per window and INV-BE-102 and the 429 boundary change. |\n\n### Assumptions\n\n| ID | Assumption | What would confirm it |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-117` | Storage conventions: `email` is trimmed and lower-cased before storage and comparison; surrogate keys are `bigint` identity; timestamps are `timestamptz` in UTC. No input states them. | The platform owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-118` | All time comparisons use the database clock. A send attempt counts while `requested_at > now() - 10 minutes`. A lock is active while `now() < locked_until`. | The requirements owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-119` | `account_locks` holds the running `failed_count`, `locked_until` and `updated_at`, one row per email. NFR-019 names locks but no counter store. | The requirements owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-120` | Verify matches the most recently issued challenge whose code equals the submitted code, then applies used, expired, success. | The requirements owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-121` | The 5th wrong code returns 401 itself and starts the lock; the lock applies from the next request. | BR-022 and BR-033 wording. |\n| `ASM-BE-122` | The challenge and send attempt are written in one transaction, held under the per-email serialisation, with the email sent before commit. A delivery failure rolls back and returns 500 `INTERNAL_ERROR`. | An email-delivery requirement (`GAP-BE-121`). |\n| `ASM-BE-123` | Codes come from a cryptographically secure random source, uniform over 000000 to 999999. BR-029 states only the format. | The security owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-124` | A missing field or a code that is not six digits returns 400 and is not counted toward the lock. | The requirements owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-125` | A successful verify consumes only the matched challenge (`GAP-BE-122`). | A decision on outstanding codes. |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-006-06"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/contract-docs-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/contract-docs-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.frontmatter | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Frontmatter\n\n```yaml\nid: contract-docs\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [Q-BE-003]\nscope: [API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307, API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943]\n```\n\n`blocked_by` names `Q-BE-003`. Until it is answered, the 401/403 documentation required by US-004-03 is not final (see `BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-008`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## Purpose\n\n**Responsible for.** The boundary comes from the project spec's module map (`backend.module-map`, row `contract-docs`). Every item below traces to a story or rule.\n\n- Publishing the OpenAPI 3.x document that covers every implemented endpoint. This includes the CRUD, tagging, search, archive and email one-time-code login (request and verify) endpoints (US-004-01, BR-012, NFR-008).\n- Documenting schemas, required and optional fields, defaults, and 4xx/5xx error responses in that document (US-004-02, US-004-03).\n- Carrying example payloads for search and tagging, including an empty-result example (US-004-05, NFR-009).\n- Carrying a data-residency handling note that covers where active and archived note data lives (US-004-06, BR-013).\n- Carrying a Scope section that lists mobile apps, attachments and real-time collaboration as excluded (US-005-01).\n- Marking interface changes: the `pinned` field and pinned-first ordering (NFR-014), and the login endpoints (NFR-015, NFR-016).\n- Computing and exposing the spec-versus-route coverage metric (US-004-04, BR-012, NFR-008).\n- Failing the docs build or CI when the document drifts from the code, or when the Scope section is missing (US-004-02, US-005-01).\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- Defining any endpoint's request, response or error contract. Each module owns its own operations, and this module renders them without restating them.\n- Refusing out-of-scope calls at runtime, and the contract tests for their absence. Both belong to `scope-guard` (BR-014 to BR-016, NFR-010, NFR-011).\n- Provisioning, backup and drift enforcement of EU residency. These belong to `residency` (BR-001 to BR-004).\n- Login behaviour (codes, lock, resend limit). It belongs to `auth`; this module only documents it.\n- Publishing a contract package, versioning it, or approving it. The orchestrator does these (core section 2).\n- Logging and classifying stakeholder requests (US-005-06, BR-017). This is a product-manager process (`GAP-BE-009`).\n- The web_app screens that consume the document (US-006-01 to US-006-06).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Data model\n\n**No database tables, columns, constraints or indexes are defined for this module.** No input requires the published document or the coverage result to be stored in PostgreSQL, and the project spec's migration plan has no migration for this module (`backend.migration-plan`). This rests on `ASM-BE-167`.\n\nThe module handles two logical artefacts. Neither is persisted by the application database.\n\n| Artefact | Content | Source of content | Stored where |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Published OpenAPI document | The whole document, in the formats under `ASM-BE-170` | The route registry and the modules' operation records (BR-012; `ASM-BE-171`) | A build and publish artefact served as-is by `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307` |\n| Coverage result | Count of documented implemented routes, count of implemented routes, the percentage, the identity of each missing route, and the evaluation outcome | The route registry compared with the published document (BR-012, US-004-04) | Computed per evaluation, not stored (`ASM-BE-167`) |\n\n- A route is identified by method plus path (`ASM-BE-174`).\n- Example payloads in the document use masked placeholders for any email address and never contain a real-looking value or a credential (core section 12).\n- The retention period of the published document and of coverage results is not stated by any input (`GAP-BE-006`). No retention claim is made.\n- The artefacts sit in the EU like every other notes_api resource (BR-001). No input places the published document anywhere else.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.state-machine | status: DERIVED -->\n## State machine\n\nNo entity in this module has a status column, so there is no persisted state machine. The coverage check does have a defined set of outcomes (US-004-04). They are listed here so every consumer reads them the same way.\n\n| State | Meaning | Reached when | Terminal |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `EVALUATING` | transient: registry and document are being compared | `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943` is called, or the CI check starts | no |\n| `COMPLETE` | every implemented route is in the document; reported as 100% | the document parses and the missing set is empty | yes |\n| `INCOMPLETE` | below 100%; each missing route is named | the document parses and the missing set is non-empty | yes |\n| `UNPARSEABLE` | the document is malformed; no percentage is reported | the document fails to parse | yes |\n\n**Transitions.**\n\n- `EVALUATING \u2192 UNPARSEABLE`. Guard: the document fails to parse. This check runs first.\n- `EVALUATING \u2192 INCOMPLETE`. Guard: the document parses and at least one implemented route is absent from it.\n- `EVALUATING \u2192 COMPLETE`. Guard: the document parses and no implemented route is absent.\n\nEach evaluation starts fresh and carries no memory of a previous one, and every outcome is terminal for that evaluation. There is therefore no illegal transition and no illegal-transition error.\n\n**Docs-build gate.** The build either passes or fails. It fails when:\n\n- the Scope section is missing (US-005-01);\n- a documented field type no longer matches the code (US-004-02).\n\nWhether `INCOMPLETE` also fails the build is not stated by any input (`GAP-BE-174`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Requirements\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-001** Serve the published document.\n- **Given** notes_api is deployed, **when** a client requests the OpenAPI document, **then** a valid OpenAPI 3.x document is returned in the requested format with status 200.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01, BR-012.\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_openapi_returns_valid_3x_document` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-002** The document lists every implemented endpoint.\n- **Given** the implemented route registry, **when** the document is returned, **then** it lists all CRUD, tagging, search, archive and email one-time-code login (request-code and verify-code) endpoints. It also lists both operations of this module.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01 (first criterion), BR-012, NFR-008.\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_every_registry_route_is_documented` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-003** Unsupported Accept header.\n- **Given** a request whose Accept header names a format this module cannot produce, **when** the document is requested, **then** the response is 406 with the registry code `NOT_ACCEPTABLE` (`backend.error-model`), and no document body is returned.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01 (third criterion).\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_unsupported_accept_returns_406` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n- **Note.** The story allows \"406 or a default-format fallback\". The 406 choice is `ASM-BE-169`. Supported formats and the default are `ASM-BE-170`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-004** New endpoints appear no later than they become callable.\n- **Given** a new endpoint is added to notes_api, **when** the document is regenerated and published, **then** the endpoint appears in the document before or at the same time it becomes callable.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01 (second criterion), BR-012.\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_ci_new_route_must_appear_in_spec` (planned).\n- **API.** none (build and CI behaviour, `ASM-BE-171`).\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-005** Required and optional fields, types and defaults.\n- **Given** an endpoint accepts a note payload or optional query parameters, **when** its schema is inspected in the document, **then** required fields, types and constraints are documented. Optional parameters are marked optional with defaults documented.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02 (first and second criteria), BR-012.\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_schemas_state_required_optional_and_defaults` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n- **Note.** Constraints are taken from the owning module and are not restated here. Constraints no module defines, such as title length and tag format, cannot be documented (`GAP-BE-171`).\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-006** Schema drift fails CI.\n- **Given** a documented field type is changed in code and the document is not updated, **when** the spec-validation check runs, **then** the check fails and names the mismatch.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02 (third criterion).\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_ci_fails_on_field_type_drift` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-007** Validation errors documented.\n- **Given** an endpoint can return validation errors, **when** the document is reviewed, **then** a 400 response with the error structure is documented. The structure is the one defined in `backend.error-model` and is not redefined here.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03 (first criterion), BR-012.\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_every_validating_endpoint_documents_400` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-008** Authentication and login refusals documented.\n- **Given** an endpoint requires authentication or authorisation, **when** the document is reviewed, **then** 401 and 403 responses are documented with their body shape. Whether any endpoint requires credentials is undecided (`Q-BE-003`), so this part stays `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`.\n- **Given** the login endpoints, **when** the document is reviewed, **then** the request-code endpoint documents 429 for more than 3 requests within 10 minutes (BR-028). The verify-code endpoint documents 423 for a locked account, 401 for a wrong code, 410 for an expired code and 409 for an already-used code (BR-033). The lock is 15 minutes after 5 wrong codes (BR-022).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03 (second criterion), BR-022, BR-028, BR-032, BR-033.\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_login_endpoints_document_429_and_423` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n- **Note.** The story attaches both the 429 and the locked-account response to \"send/verify\". BR-032 says request-code is not refused during a lock. This is `CON-BE-154`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-009** Documented 404 matches actual behaviour.\n- **Given** an endpoint that can return 404, **when** the documented 404 response is compared with the actual response, **then** they match.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03 (third criterion; its precondition text is garbled, `GAP-BE-170`).\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_documented_404_matches_actual_responses` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-010** Example payloads.\n- **Given** the search endpoint and the tagging endpoints, **when** a developer views the document, **then** each has an example request with its query parameters or body and an example response body. Search also has an example of an empty result.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-05 (both criteria), NFR-009.\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_search_and_tagging_have_examples_including_empty` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-011** Data-residency handling note.\n- **Given** the published document, **when** an operations engineer reads the data-handling note, **then** it states that all note data is stored and served within the EU. Any difference in storage location between archived and active notes is called out. The current inputs describe none, because archiving is a flag on the same record (BR-007).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-06 (both criteria), BR-013, BR-001, BR-007.\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_residency_note_states_eu_and_archive_location` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n- **Note.** The note covers note data only. It makes no claim about the login-email address leaving the EU, because NFR-017 is garbled on that point (`GAP-BE-003`).\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-012** Scope section content.\n- **Given** the published document, **when** a reader views its Scope section, **then** mobile apps, attachments and real-time collaboration are listed as explicitly excluded.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01 (first criterion), BR-014, BR-015, BR-016.\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_scope_section_lists_three_exclusions` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-013** Scope changes need review.\n- **Given** a documentation update adds or changes an out-of-scope item, **when** it is proposed, **then** it requires review and approval before merge.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01 (second criterion).\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_scope_change_requires_review` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Note.** The review mechanism is not named by any input (`GAP-BE-173`).\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-014** Missing Scope section fails the docs build.\n- **Given** a docs build, **when** the Scope section is missing, **then** the build fails with a lint or validation error.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01 (third criterion).\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_build_fails_without_scope_section` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-015** Full coverage.\n- **Given** all implemented endpoints are documented, **when** the coverage check runs, **then** it reports 100%.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04 (first criterion), BR-012, NFR-008.\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_coverage_reports_100_when_all_documented` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-016** Partial coverage names the missing endpoint.\n- **Given** one implemented endpoint is missing from the document, **when** the coverage check runs, **then** it reports less than 100% and names the missing endpoint.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04 (second criterion), BR-012.\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_coverage_names_missing_endpoint` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-017** Malformed document.\n- **Given** the document is malformed, **when** the coverage check runs, **then** it fails with a clear parsing error and reports no percentage. The failure is 500 with the code `SPEC_MALFORMED` (`ASM-BE-173`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04 (third criterion).\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_coverage_fails_on_malformed_spec_without_false_percent` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943`.\n\n**BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-018** Interface changes are marked.\n- **Given** the `pinned` field, the pinned-first ordering, and the email one-time-code login endpoints, **when** the document is reviewed, **then** each is marked as an interface change that consumers must adapt to. The login endpoints are marked additive, because existing login methods are unchanged.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-014, NFR-015, NFR-016, BR-032.\n- **Verification.** `contractdocs_interface_changes_are_marked` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307`.\n- **Note.** How consumers are notified is not defined (`GAP-BE-173`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307` | GET | `/openapi` | `contract-docs` | 200 | 406, 500 | Return the published OpenAPI 3.x document covering every implemented endpoint, with schemas, 4xx/5xx errors, examples, the data-residency note and the Scope section. Format is chosen by the Accept header; an unsupported Accept returns 406 or falls back to the default format (US-004-01). |\n| `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943` | GET | `/openapi/coverage` | `contract-docs` | 200 | 500 | Return the spec-versus-route coverage check: the share of implemented routes present in the published document (US-004-04, NFR-008). |\n\n### `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307` \u2014 GET `/openapi`\n\nReturn the published OpenAPI 3.x document covering every implemented endpoint, with schemas, 4xx/5xx errors, examples, the data-residency note and the Scope section. Format is chosen by the Accept header; an unsupported Accept returns 406 or falls back to the default format (US-004-01).\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `openapi` (string, required): OpenAPI 3.x version string of the document.\n- `info` (object, required): API title and version, including the data-residency note (US-004-06) and the Scope section (US-005-01: no attachments, no real-time channel, no mobile-specific endpoints).\n- `paths` (object, required): Every implemented endpoint with parameters, request and response schemas, 4xx/5xx error responses and example payloads (US-004-02, US-004-03, US-004-05).\n- `components` (object, required): Shared schemas, including the error envelope.\n\n### `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943` \u2014 GET `/openapi/coverage`\n\nReturn the spec-versus-route coverage check: the share of implemented routes present in the published document (US-004-04, NFR-008).\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `coverage_percent` (number, required): Percentage of implemented endpoints documented in the published spec; target 100.\n- `implemented_count` (integer, required): Number of endpoints registered in the route registry.\n- `documented_count` (integer, required): Number of those endpoints present in the published OpenAPI document.\n- `undocumented_endpoints` (array, required): Method and path of each implemented endpoint missing from the spec; empty when coverage is 100%.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.invariants | status: DERIVED -->\n## Invariants\n\n- **INV-BE-151.** The coverage check reports 100% only when the document parsed successfully and no implemented route is missing from it. It never reports 100% by default or on failure. Derivation: US-004-04 criteria 1 and 3, BR-012.\n- **INV-BE-152.** A malformed document never produces a coverage percentage. Derivation: US-004-04 (third criterion).\n- **INV-BE-153.** Every published document contains a Scope section listing mobile apps, attachments and real-time collaboration as excluded. A build without it fails and is not published. Derivation: US-005-01.\n- **INV-BE-154.** The statuses and error responses documented for an endpoint equal what the endpoint actually returns. Derivation: US-004-03 (third criterion), US-004-02 (third criterion).\n- **INV-BE-155.** The residency note states EU storage and serving for note data and never names a location outside the EU for it. Derivation: US-004-06, BR-001, BR-013.\n- **INV-BE-156.** Neither operation changes any state. Both are GET operations over derived data, so repeating either returns the same result for the same registry and document (`ASM-BE-167`).\n- **INV-BE-157.** No example, note, or published artefact contains a credential, a real-looking email address or other PII. Derivation: core section 12.\n- **INV-BE-158.** A path refused as out of scope is never listed as an implemented endpoint, and is not counted in the coverage figure (`ASM-BE-172`). Derivation: BR-014 to BR-016, US-005-02.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Authorization\n\nNo input defines roles, and the project's `backend.auth-model` is `BLOCKED` on `Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-002` and `Q-BE-003`. The permission matrix below is therefore minimal.\n\n| Operation | Caller | Allowed |\n|---|---|---|\n| `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307` | any caller (`ASM-BE-168`) | yes |\n| `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943` | any caller (`ASM-BE-168`, default taken for `Q-BE-154`) | yes |\n\n- **Intended consumers.** Per the stories these are integration developers building the web_app, backend developers maintaining notes_api, and operations engineers reviewing residency (US-004-01, US-004-04, US-004-06).\n- **Ownership predicate.** None. Neither resource is user-scoped, and both read system-level artefacts.\n- **401 and 403.** Neither operation emits them while `ASM-BE-168` stands. If `Q-BE-003` resolves to credentials being required on every endpoint, this section and `BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-008` must be revised.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Jobs\n\n**Scheduled work and sweeps.** None. No input requires periodic regeneration, retention sweeps or retries for this module.\n\n**Build and CI steps.** These run on build, not on a timer. The pipeline tool is not named by any input (`GAP-BE-175`).\n\n| Step | Fails when | Source |\n|---|---|---|\n| Regenerate and publish the document from the route registry and operation records | the document cannot be generated | US-004-01 (second criterion), `ASM-BE-171` |\n| Spec-validation check | a documented field type disagrees with the code | US-004-02 (third criterion) |\n| Scope-section lint | the Scope section is missing | US-005-01 (third criterion) |\n| Coverage check | the document is malformed. Whether `INCOMPLETE` also fails the build is open (`GAP-BE-174`). | US-004-04, BR-012 |\n\n**Retries.** None specified.\n\n**Idempotency.** Both operations are GET. They are idempotent, change no state, and need no idempotency key.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Test plan\n\n**Unit.**\n- Accept negotiation maps to a supported format or to 406 (`BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-003`).\n- Coverage computation returns `COMPLETE`, `INCOMPLETE` or `UNPARSEABLE` for fixtures with no routes missing, one route missing, and a malformed document (FR-015 to FR-017).\n- The route identity key is method plus path (`ASM-BE-174`).\n- Examples contain only masked placeholders (INV-BE-157).\n\n**Integration.**\n- `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307` returns a valid OpenAPI 3.x document in each supported format (FR-001).\n- `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943` reports 100%, below 100% with the missing route named, and the parse-error failure (FR-015 to FR-017).\n- The registry-versus-document comparison includes this module's own two routes and excludes refused out-of-scope paths (FR-002, INV-BE-158).\n\n**Contract.**\n- The document passes OpenAPI 3.x validation, and every implemented route appears in it (FR-002, FR-004).\n- Documented statuses, including 404, 400 and the login refusals, equal actual responses (FR-007 to FR-009, INV-BE-154).\n- The Scope section is present, and its absence fails the build (FR-012, FR-014).\n- The residency note and the interface-change markings are present (FR-011, FR-018).\n- Search and tagging examples exist, including the empty-result one (FR-010).\n\nTest names are proposed under each requirement in `backend.contract-docs.requirements`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.gaps | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Gaps\n\nIDs minted by this module are defined below. IDs from the project spec are cited and not redefined.\n\n### Questions\n\n- **Q-BE-154** (`degradable`). Should `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943` be access-restricted, given it exposes the route inventory and any undocumented routes? Default taken, unattended: it follows the same access rule as `API-CONTRACTDOCS-304307` (`ASM-BE-168`).\n- **Q-BE-155** (`degradable`). For an unsupported Accept header, should the response be 406 or a default-format fallback? Default taken, unattended: 406 (`ASM-BE-169`).\n\n### Contradictions\n\n- **CON-BE-154** (non-critical).\n  - **Claim A.** US-004-03 (second criterion) says the send and verify login endpoints document a 429 for more than 3 requests within 10 minutes and a locked-account response after 5 wrong codes.\n  - **Claim B.** BR-028 attaches 429 to requests for a code. BR-032 says that during a lock only verification is refused, and BR-033 attaches 423 to verify-code.\n  - **Reading used.** Request-code documents 429 and no lock refusal. Verify-code documents the lock refusal (423) and no 429.\n  - **Consequence of the other side.** Documenting 429 on verify-code or a lock refusal on request-code would misstate behaviour that `auth` implements.\n\n### Assumptions\n\n- **ASM-BE-167.** No database table is needed. The published document is a build artefact, and the coverage result is derived per request and not stored. Confirm with the platform owner.\n- **ASM-BE-168.** Both operations are read-only and callable without credentials, pending `Q-BE-003`. This also covers the default taken for `Q-BE-154`.\n- **ASM-BE-169.** An unsupported Accept returns 406 with `NOT_ACCEPTABLE`, using the proposed registry in `backend.error-model` (`ASM-BE-001`). This is the default taken for `Q-BE-155`.\n- **ASM-BE-170.** The supported formats are JSON and YAML, because US-004-01 names \"JSON/YAML\". JSON is the default when no Accept header is sent. No input states the default.\n- **ASM-BE-171.** The document is generated at build and publish time from the route registry and the operation records, then served as-is. US-004-01 speaks of the spec being \"regenerated/published\", and BR-012 names the registry as the comparison source.\n- **ASM-BE-172.** \"Implemented endpoints\" means routes that perform an operation. Paths refused with `OUT_OF_SCOPE` by `scope-guard` are not implemented endpoints. This module's own two routes are implemented endpoints and are counted.\n- **ASM-BE-173.** A malformed document makes `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943` return 500 with the proposed code `SPEC_MALFORMED`. The failure is server-side, since the artefact is the service's own.\n- **ASM-BE-174.** A route is identified by method plus path, and coverage is the documented implemented routes divided by the implemented routes, as a percentage.\n- **ASM-BE-175.** The Scope section is a named part of the document's top-level descriptive content. Review before merge is a repository review gate.\n\n### Gaps\n\n- **GAP-BE-170.** The precondition of US-004-03's third criterion is garbled. It reads \"a documented field type is changed in code\" but then checks the 404 response. Reading used: the documented 404 must match the actual one (`BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-009`). Closing artefact: clean story text.\n- **GAP-BE-171.** US-004-02 mentions constraints such as title length and tag format, but no input defines them (see also `GAP-BE-010`). The document can only state constraints a module defines. Closing artefact: a requirement giving field constraints.\n- **GAP-BE-172.** No latency, throughput or availability target exists for the two operations of this module. NFR-013 covers CRUD and search only. Closing artefact: an NFR input.\n- **GAP-BE-173.** The mechanism for \"consumers are notified that they must adapt\" (NFR-014 to NFR-016) and the review and approval mechanism for Scope changes (US-005-01) are not defined. Closing artefact: a process or tooling input.\n- **GAP-BE-174.** Not stated: whether coverage below 100% blocks the build or only reports, and how a documented entry with no matching route is treated. The coverage definition runs in one direction only. Closing artefact: a rule from the BR-012 owner.\n- **GAP-BE-175.** No input names the CI or docs-build tooling, the OpenAPI validator, or the exact OpenAPI 3.x minor version. `platform.md` names none. Closing artefact: a platform input.\n\n### Cited from the project spec\n\n- `GAP-BE-001`: the four inbound web_app handoffs were listed but their contents were not supplied, so this module could not read or answer them. The lane-needs step must record each as answered or unanswerable.\n- `GAP-BE-003`: NFR-017 is garbled on whether the email address leaves the EU (`BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-011`).\n- `GAP-BE-005`: no operation lists active or archived notes, so the document cannot show one.\n- `GAP-BE-006`: encryption at rest and retention of residency artefacts are unstated.\n- `GAP-BE-009`: US-005-06 is a product-manager process.\n- `GAP-BE-010`: tag rules are unstated.\n- `Q-BE-003`: what a successful verify issues, and whether endpoints require it. It blocks the 401/403 documentation.\n- `ASM-BE-001`: the error envelope and registry names are proposals.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-004-01", "US-004-02", "US-004-03", "US-004-04", "US-004-05", "US-004-06", "US-005-01"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/scope-guard-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/scope-guard-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guard.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n## Frontmatter\n\n```yaml\nid: scope-guard\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: []\nscope: []   # no operation records were supplied for this module; it owns no API- id\n```\n\n`scope` is empty because the list of operation records supplied for this module is empty. The project spec's module map describes `scope-guard` as request middleware over the whole HTTP surface (`backend.module-map`). `contract_pin` is omitted because the backend is the source.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guard.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guard.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** `scope-guard` is the middleware that keeps `notes_api` inside its documented scope. It does this in four ways:\n\n- It refuses calls to attachment, real-time and mobile-specific endpoints with a structured, documented error (BR-016, US-005-04, NFR-011).\n- It makes sure no attachment or file-upload endpoint exists and no multipart file payload is accepted (BR-014, US-005-02).\n- It makes sure no WebSocket, SSE or other real-time channel exists and that none is established (BR-015, US-005-03).\n- It owns the automated guardrail contract test, run on every CI build, that fails when any endpoint in an excluded category appears (NFR-010, US-005-05).\n\n**Stories owned:** US-005-02, US-005-03, US-005-04, US-005-05.\n\n**Not responsible for:**\n\n- The published OpenAPI document and its Scope section (US-005-01). `contract-docs` owns them. This module only supplies the exclusion list that the Scope section describes.\n- Logging and classifying stakeholder requests against the exclusion list (US-005-06, BR-017). That is a product-manager process (`GAP-BE-009`). The run-time refusal event in FR-009 is a different thing from that log.\n- The outcome of concurrent edits to one note. `notes` owns the PATCH behaviour. This module owns only the absence of live broadcast and merge logic (FR-005; `GAP-BE-221`).\n- Authentication and authorisation (`backend.auth-model`, which is BLOCKED).\n- The full error envelope and registry (`backend.error-model`). This module uses them and does not redefine them.\n- Residency, which is `residency`'s concern.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guard.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guard.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Data model\n\n**This module has no database tables, columns or indexes, and no migration.** `backend.migration-plan` lists no `scope-guard` migration, and `backend.data-lifecycle` lists no `scope-guard` data. No request is persisted. The module is stateless per request, and a refused request writes nothing (US-005-02, multipart criterion).\n\nThe module does need an **exclusion list**: the set of request patterns it refuses. No input says where it is stored. `ASM-BE-217` assumes it is a versioned configuration artefact in the repository. The runtime classifier and the CI guardrail test both read it, so the two cannot drift apart.\n\n### Logical shape of one exclusion entry\n\n| Field | Type | Nullable | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `category` | enum `attachment` / `real-time` / `mobile` | no | none | Categories come from BR-014, BR-015 and BR-016 |\n| `method` | HTTP method | yes | null, meaning any method (`ASM-BE-222`) | none |\n| `path_pattern` | text | no | none | Segments in braces match exactly one path segment.
