The entry also matches any deeper path beneath it (`ASM-BE-223`). |\n| `reason` | text | no | none | Human-readable explanation that names the scope exclusion (US-005-04) |\n\nThe pair (`method`, `path_pattern`) is unique.\n\n### Seed entries\n\n| category | method | path_pattern | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| attachment | any | `/notes/{id}/attachments` | US-005-02, example path |\n| attachment | any | `/attachments` | US-005-05, example |\n| real-time | any | `/ws` | US-005-05, example |\n| mobile | none | none | No input gives any mobile-specific pattern (`GAP-BE-220`) |\n\nProtocol signals for real-time connections (an upgrade request, an event-stream request) are fixed in code and not configured (`ASM-BE-220`).\n\nPII: the entries carry no personal data.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guard.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guard.state-machine | status: DERIVED -->\n## State machine\n\n**No entity owned by this module has a status column, so there is no entity state machine.** The module makes one decision per request. Nothing persists between requests.\n\n| From | Trigger and guard | To |\n|---|---|---|\n| Received | The request path matches an exclusion entry (`ASM-BE-222`, `ASM-BE-223`) | Refused: path (attachment, real-time or mobile) |\n| Received | No path match, and the request carries a real-time protocol signal (`ASM-BE-220`) | Refused: real-time |\n| Received | No match so far, and the request carries a multipart file payload (`ASM-BE-219`) | Refused: media type |\n| Received | None of the above | Passed to routing |\n\nWithin this module the guards are evaluated in the order shown: path, then protocol signal, then media type (`ASM-BE-224`).\n\n**Terminal states.** All three Refused states are terminal. The request ends with the refusal response and no side effect. Passed to routing is terminal for this module, and the request then belongs to the owning module.\n\n**Illegal transitions.** None exist, because there is no persisted state to transition. A refusal is never reversed within a request.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guard.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guard.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Requirements\n\nAll `API.` entries below are \"none\" because this module owns no operation. Routes are referred to by method and path.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARD-FR-001: attachment paths are refused\n- **Given** a request, with any HTTP method, to a path that matches an `attachment` exclusion entry, for example `POST /notes/{id}/attachments`,\n- **When** the request is received,\n- **Then** the response status is 404, the body is the structured error with the registry code `OUT_OF_SCOPE` and a message referencing the scope exclusion, and nothing is persisted.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-02 (first criterion), US-005-04 (first criterion), BR-014, BR-016, NFR-011. The reading \"404 status with a descriptive body\" resolves `CON-BE-002` (`ASM-BE-016`).\n- **Verification.** `scope_guard.attachment_path_returns_404_out_of_scope` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARD-FR-002: multipart file payloads are refused\n- **Given** a request with a `multipart/form-data` file payload sent to `POST /notes` or to `PATCH /notes/{id}` (`ASM-BE-219` extends this to every endpoint that takes a body),\n- **When** the request is received,\n- **Then** the response status is 415, the body is a structured error with a code and a human-readable reason that references the scope exclusion, and no file content is persisted.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-02 (second criterion), US-005-04 (second criterion), BR-014, NFR-011.\n- **Verification.** `scope_guard.multipart_upload_returns_415_no_persist` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Open.** The code is `UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE`, taken from the project error registry. US-005-04 gives `OUT_OF_SCOPE` only as an example (`ASM-BE-218`).\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARD-FR-003: real-time connections are never established\n- **Given** a client attempts a WebSocket upgrade or an SSE connection to any `notes_api` endpoint,\n- **When** the request is received,\n- **Then** the connection is rejected, no protocol upgrade occurs, and a standard error status is returned. The body follows the error envelope.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-03 (first criterion), BR-015, US-005-04.\n- **Verification.** `scope_guard.websocket_upgrade_rejected_no_101` and `scope_guard.sse_request_rejected` (both planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Open.** The exact status is not defined by any input (`GAP-BE-011`). Detection of the signals is `ASM-BE-220`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARD-FR-004: mobile-specific endpoints are refused\n- **Given** a request to a path that matches a `mobile` exclusion entry,\n- **When** the request is received,\n- **Then** the response is the same structured refusal as FR-001, referencing the scope exclusion.\n- **Traces to.** BR-016, US-005-04 (first criterion), NFR-011.\n- **Verification.** `scope_guard.mobile_pattern_refused` (planned). It cannot be written until `GAP-BE-220` is closed.\n- **API.** none.\n- **Open.** No input defines any mobile-specific pattern, so the seed list has no mobile entry.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARD-FR-005: no live broadcast or merge logic on concurrent edits\n- **Given** two clients send `PATCH /notes/{id}` to the same note concurrently,\n- **When** both requests are processed,\n- **Then** each gets the standard response of the `notes` module, and no component in `notes_api` broadcasts the change to other clients or merges the two edits.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-03 (second criterion), BR-015.\n- **Verification.** `scope_guard.concurrent_patch_no_broadcast_no_merge` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Open.** The inputs say \"last-write/conflict\" without choosing one. That choice belongs to `notes` (`GAP-BE-221`).\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARD-FR-006: every refusal is a documented 4xx with an explanatory message\n- **Given** any refusal produced by this module,\n- **When** the response is built,\n- **Then** it uses the error envelope and registry of `backend.error-model`, carries a machine-readable code and a message naming the scope exclusion, and its 4xx status is documented in the published spec.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-011 (100% of out-of-scope calls return a documented 4xx error with an explanatory message), BR-016, US-005-04.\n- **Verification.** `scope_guard.refusals_use_error_envelope` (planned).\n- **API.** none. The documentation of these responses is `contract-docs`'s section, not restated here.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARD-FR-007: the guardrail test passes only when no excluded endpoint exists\n- **Given** the CI pipeline runs,\n- **When** the scope-guardrail test suite executes against the OpenAPI document,\n- **Then** it passes only if zero endpoints match the excluded categories (mobile, attachment, real-time). The detected count must be 0 on every build.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-05 (first criterion), NFR-010, BR-014, BR-015.\n- **Verification.** `scope_guard.contract_suite_zero_excluded_endpoints` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Dependency.** The published document must not list the refused paths as endpoints, or the suite would fail by construction. The refusal behaviour is documented through the shared error response and the Scope section, owned by `contract-docs`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARD-FR-008: a new excluded endpoint fails CI and blocks the merge\n- **Given** a developer adds an endpoint matching an excluded pattern, for example `/attachments` or `/ws`,\n- **When** CI runs on the pull request,\n- **Then** the guardrail test fails and the merge is blocked.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-05 (second criterion), NFR-010.\n- **Verification.** `scope_guard.injected_excluded_path_fails_suite` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Open.** How the merge is blocked depends on the CI host, which no input names (`GAP-BE-223`).\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARD-FR-009: each refusal produces an event\n- **Given** this module refuses a request,\n- **When** the response is sent,\n- **Then** an out-of-scope-refusal event is logged, as `backend.observability` already lists.\n- **Traces to.** `backend.observability`. It supports the metric context of BR-017 but does not replace the product-manager log (`GAP-BE-009`).\n- **Verification.** `scope_guard.refusal_emits_event` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Open.** The fields of the event are not specified by any input (`GAP-BE-008`). They must exclude any one-time code and any unmasked email address (`backend.observability`, `ASM-BE-014`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guard.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guard.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\nNo HTTP operation is defined.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guard.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guard.invariants | status: DERIVED -->\n## Invariants\n\n- **INV-BE-201.** After every build, the OpenAPI document and the route registry contain no endpoint in an excluded category (attachment, real-time, mobile). *Source: BR-014, BR-015, NFR-010, US-005-05.*\n- **INV-BE-202.** After any request this module refuses, no file content, note, tag or other record was written because of that request. *Source: US-005-02, second criterion.*\n- **INV-BE-203.** Every refusal this module sends carries a machine-readable code and a human-readable reason that references the scope exclusion. It is never a bare 404 and never a silent failure. *Source: BR-016, US-005-04, NFR-011.*\n- **INV-BE-204.** No protocol upgrade and no event-stream connection is ever established on any `notes_api` endpoint. *Source: US-005-03, BR-015.*\n- **INV-BE-205.** Refusing a request never changes any state, and the same request gets the same verdict from the same exclusion list. The decision is a pure function of the request and the list. *Derived from the stateless design; see `backend.scope-guard.state-machine`.*\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guard.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guard.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Authorization\n\n**Operations and roles.** This module owns no operation, so there is no role \u00d7 operation matrix. The refusal verdict is not tied to a role. `ASM-BE-221` assumes the module runs before authentication and validation, so every caller, authenticated or not, gets the same verdict for an excluded request.\n\n**Ownership predicate.** None. The module handles no user-scoped resource.\n\n**Relationship to `backend.auth-model`.** That section is BLOCKED (`Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-002`, `Q-BE-003`). Nothing in this module waits on those decisions as long as `ASM-BE-221` holds. If the human decides that unauthenticated callers must get 401 before any other response, FR-001 to FR-004 change order, and that is a decision for the human.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guard.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guard.jobs | status: DERIVED -->\n## Jobs\n\n- **Scheduled work and sweeps.** None. The module stores nothing that needs a sweep, and `backend.data-lifecycle` lists no `scope-guard` data.\n- **Retries.** None. A refusal is final and needs no retry.\n- **Idempotency keys.** None. The classifier is a pure function of the request, so repeating a request gives the same refusal.\n- **CI execution.** The guardrail contract test is the only recurring execution. It runs on every CI build and on every pull request (NFR-010, US-005-05). It is a pipeline step, not a scheduled job. The CI host is not named by any input (`GAP-BE-223`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guard.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guard.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Test plan\n\nAll test names are proposals, marked \"(planned)\" in the requirements.\n\n### Unit\n- The classifier matches `/notes/{id}/attachments`, `/attachments` and `/ws` for any method. *(FR-001, FR-003)*\n- The classifier does not match in-scope paths such as `/notes`, `/notes/search`, `/notes/{id}/tags`, `/auth/request-code`, `/auth/verify-code` or `/openapi`. *(FR-001)*\n- A deeper path beneath an excluded pattern matches (`ASM-BE-223`). *(FR-001)*\n- Evaluation order is path, then protocol signal, then media type (`ASM-BE-224`). *(state machine)*\n- Detection of an upgrade signal and an event-stream signal (`ASM-BE-220`). *(FR-003)*\n- A refusal body has a code and a message that references the scope exclusion. *(FR-006)*\n\n### Integration\n- `POST /notes/{id}/attachments` returns 404 with the refusal body. *(FR-001)*\n- A multipart file upload to `POST /notes` and to `PATCH /notes/{id}` returns 415, and the notes tables are unchanged afterwards. *(FR-002, INV-BE-202)*\n- A WebSocket upgrade request is rejected and no upgrade is performed. An SSE request is rejected. *(FR-003)*\n- Two concurrent `PATCH /notes/{id}` requests each get the `notes` module's standard response, with no broadcast. *(FR-005)*\n- A refusal emits an out-of-scope-refusal event that contains no code and no unmasked email. *(FR-009)*\n\n### Contract\n- The suite runs against `contracts/openapi.yaml` and passes only when zero endpoints match an excluded category. *(FR-007, INV-BE-201)*\n- Negative test: inject `/attachments` and `/ws` paths into a copy of the document and confirm the suite fails. *(FR-008)*\n- Every refusal response in the document is documented with the shared error envelope. *(FR-006, NFR-011)*\n- The detected out-of-scope endpoint count is exported per build, as NFR-010 requires.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guard.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guard.gaps | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Gaps\n\n### Gaps raised by this module\n\n- **`GAP-BE-220`** (non-critical) \u2014 No input defines any mobile-specific endpoint pattern, and the attachment and real-time patterns are only examples (`/notes/{id}/attachments`, `/attachments`, `/ws`). *Degrades:* FR-004 and the data-model seed list. *Done instead:* seeded with the cited examples and left the mobile category empty. *Closing artefact:* the exclusion list that the Scope section describes (US-005-01), or a product decision.\n- **`GAP-BE-221`** (non-critical) \u2014 US-005-03 says concurrent PATCH requests get a \"last-write/conflict\" response without choosing one. *Degrades:* FR-005. *Done instead:* this module asserts only that no broadcast or merge logic exists. *Closing artefact:* a decision in the `notes` module on concurrent-edit behaviour.\n- **`GAP-BE-222`** (non-critical) \u2014 No input defines the response to a request for a path that matches no exclusion entry and no route. BR-016 forbids a bare 404 only for out-of-scope calls, and the registry has no generic not-found code. *Degrades:* the \"Passed to routing\" boundary. *Done instead:* nothing is specified. *Closing artefact:* an error-handling requirement for unknown routes.\n- **`GAP-BE-223`** (non-critical) \u2014 No input names the CI host or says how a failing check blocks a merge. `platform.md` names AWS only. *Degrades:* FR-008, the CI part of `backend.scope-guard.jobs`. *Done instead:* the requirement states the outcome (the guardrail fails and the merge is blocked) without a mechanism. *Closing artefact:* a platform input naming the CI system and its merge rules.\n\n### Carried from the project spec (cited, not re-raised)\n\n`CON-BE-002`, `GAP-BE-008`, `GAP-BE-009`, `GAP-BE-011`, `ASM-BE-001`, `ASM-BE-014`, `ASM-BE-016`. `Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-002` and `Q-BE-003` are blocking items for `backend.auth-model`, not for this module (`ASM-BE-221`).\n\n### Assumptions\n\n- **`ASM-BE-217`** \u2014 The exclusion list is a versioned configuration artefact in the repository, not a database table. No input names its storage. It is read by both the runtime classifier and the CI test. *Confirm with:* the platform owner.\n- **`ASM-BE-218`** \u2014 A multipart file upload returns 415 with the registry code `UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE`, and the message references the scope exclusion. US-005-04 gives `OUT_OF_SCOPE` only as an example (\"e.g.\"). *Confirm with:* the contract owner. If `OUT_OF_SCOPE` with 415 is wanted, the registry in `backend.error-model` changes.\n- **`ASM-BE-219`** \u2014 The multipart refusal applies to every endpoint that takes a body, not only note create and update. No endpoint accepts files (BR-014), so a broader rule has no conflicting case. *Confirm with:* the contract owner.\n- **`ASM-BE-220`** \u2014 Real-time attempts are detected by an `Upgrade: websocket` header and an `Accept: text/event-stream` header, on any path. No input says how detection works. *Confirm with:* the platform owner.\n- **`ASM-BE-221`** \u2014 The module runs before authentication and validation, so the verdict is the same for every caller. This keeps the work independent of the BLOCKED auth model. *Confirm with:* the human, together with `Q-BE-003`.\n- **`ASM-BE-222`** \u2014 An exclusion entry with no method applies to every HTTP method. US-005-02 shows a POST example, and BR-014 forbids all attachment endpoints.\n- **`ASM-BE-223`** \u2014 A `path_pattern` also matches any deeper path beneath it, and `{\u2026}` matches exactly one segment.\n- **`ASM-BE-224`** \u2014 Evaluation order inside the module is path, then protocol signal, then media type. A multipart request to `/notes/{id}/attachments` therefore gets the 404 refusal.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guard.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-005-02", "US-005-03", "US-005-04", "US-005-05"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/residency-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/residency-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.frontmatter | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Frontmatter\n\n```yaml\nid: residency\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [GAP-BE-270, GAP-BE-271]\nscope: []\n```\n\n`scope:` is empty because no operation records, and therefore no minted `API-` ids, were supplied for this module (`GAP-BE-270`). Operations are referred to below by method and path, with `ASM-BE-267` as the basis.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## Purpose\n\n**Responsible for.** The `residency` module keeps and evaluates the evidence that notes_api infrastructure is in approved EU regions. Source: module map in `backend.module-map`; BR-001 to BR-004; NFR-001 to NFR-004.\n\n- Deciding whether a provisioning request names an approved EU region (US-001-01).\n- Validating that a backup configuration is EU-only before any backup runs (US-001-02).\n- Evaluating a deployment against the CI residency gate, failing closed on missing region metadata (US-001-03).\n- Producing the compliance report: every resource, its region, its pass/fail or unverified status, and the percent compliant (US-001-04).\n- Deriving the launch gate from that report (US-001-05).\n- Running the post-launch drift scan and raising an alert to the DevOps team when a non-EU resource is found (US-001-06).\n\n**Operations.** The prior approved BACKEND-SPEC (attached to `feedback.md`, CR-131) lists these operations by method and path. `ASM-BE-267` explains why they are not given ids here.\n\n- `POST /residency/provisioning-requests`\n- `POST /residency/backup-validations`\n- `POST /residency/ci-gate-checks`\n- `GET /residency/compliance-report`\n- `GET /residency/launch-gate`\n- `POST /residency/drift-scans`\n- `GET /residency/drift-scans/latest`\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- Creating, moving or deleting cloud resources. Region constraints are infrastructure-as-code (BR-001, `backend.migration-plan`), and this module records and decides but does not provision (`ASM-BE-275`).\n- Writing backups. This module judges a backup configuration only.\n- Note, search, login or OpenAPI behaviour. Those belong to `notes`, `search`, `auth` and `contract-docs`.\n- Documenting the data-residency note in the published OpenAPI document (`contract-docs`, US-004-06).\n- The Engineering Lead's human sign-off process outside the API (BR-003; `GAP-BE-277`).\n- Any real-time or push channel to the DevOps team beyond recording that an alert was raised (`GAP-BE-275`).\n\n**Dependencies.** None on other modules (module map). It is not on the request path of notes or search (`ASM-BE-013` of the project spec).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Data model\n\nPostgreSQL 16 in an EU region (`platform.md`; BR-001). Tables live in a `residency` schema in the same database (`ASM-BE-268`). Ids are UUIDs and timestamps are `timestamptz` in UTC (`ASM-BE-269`). No column holds PII or a secret.\n\n**`residency.approved_regions`**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `region_code` | text | NOT NULL | | PRIMARY KEY |\n| `active` | boolean | NOT NULL | `true` | |\n| `added_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | `now()` | |\n\nThe contents are not supplied (`GAP-BE-272`). The primary key serves exact-match lookups.\n\n**`residency.resources`** (the inventory)\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | uuid | NOT NULL | generated | PRIMARY KEY |\n| `resource_ref` | text | NOT NULL | | UNIQUE |\n| `resource_type` | text | NOT NULL | | |\n| `region` | text | NULL | | NULL means missing or unreadable |\n| `verification_status` | text | NOT NULL | `'unverified'` | CHECK in (`verified`, `non_compliant`, `unverified`) |\n| `last_verified_at` | timestamptz | NULL | | |\n| `created_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | `now()` | |\n\nIndex on `verification_status` serves the percent-compliant count.\n\n**`residency.provisioning_requests`**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | uuid | NOT NULL | generated | PRIMARY KEY |\n| `resource_type` | text | NOT NULL | | |\n| `requested_region` | text | NOT NULL | | |\n| `decision` | text | NOT NULL | | CHECK in (`accepted`, `rejected`) |\n| `rejection_reason` | text | NULL | | |\n| `resource_id` | uuid | NULL | | FK `resources(id)` ON DELETE RESTRICT |\n| `decided_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | `now()` | |\n\n`CHECK (decision <> 'rejected' OR resource_id IS NULL)` enforces `INV-BE-252`. Index on `decided_at` serves history queries.\n\n**`residency.backup_validations`**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | uuid | NOT NULL | generated | PRIMARY KEY |\n| `backup_target` | text | NOT NULL | | |\n| `primary_region` | text | NOT NULL | | |\n| `replica_regions` | text[] | NOT NULL | `'{}'` | |\n| `valid` | boolean | NOT NULL | | |\n| `violating_regions` | text[] | NOT NULL | `'{}'` | CHECK (`valid` = (cardinality(`violating_regions`) = 0)) |\n| `duration_ms` | integer | NOT NULL | | CHECK (`duration_ms` >= 0) |\n| `validated_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | `now()` | |\n\nIndex on `validated_at`.\n\n**`residency.ci_gate_checks`**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | uuid | NOT NULL | generated | PRIMARY KEY |\n| `deployment_id` | text | NOT NULL | | |\n| `target_regions` | text[] | NOT NULL | | |\n| `verdict` | text | NOT NULL | | CHECK in (`pass`, `fail`) |\n| `violations` | jsonb | NOT NULL | `'[]'` | CHECK (`verdict` = 'fail' OR `violations` = '[]') |\n| `checked_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | `now()` | |\n\nIndex on `deployment_id`. It is not unique, because a re-check creates a new row (`ASM-BE-277`).\n\n**`residency.compliance_reports`**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | uuid | NOT NULL | generated | PRIMARY KEY |\n| `generated_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | `now()` | |\n| `total_resources` | integer | NOT NULL | | CHECK (>= 0) |\n| `verified_count` | integer | NOT NULL | | CHECK (between 0 and `total_resources`) |\n| `percent_compliant` | numeric(5,2) | NOT NULL | | CHECK (between 0 and 100) |\n\nScale 2 is proposed (`ASM-BE-271`). Index on `generated_at` serves the \"latest report\" lookup.\n\n**`residency.compliance_report_items`**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `report_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | | FK `compliance_reports(id)` ON DELETE RESTRICT |\n| `resource_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | | FK `resources(id)` ON DELETE RESTRICT |\n| `region` | text | NULL | | snapshot at generation |\n| `status` | text | NOT NULL | | CHECK in (`verified`, `non_compliant`, `unverified`) |\n\nPRIMARY KEY (`report_id`, `resource_id`); the key's leading column serves per-report listing.\n\n**`residency.launch_gate_evaluations`**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | uuid | NOT NULL | generated | PRIMARY KEY |\n| `report_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | | FK `compliance_reports(id)` ON DELETE RESTRICT |\n| `status` | text | NOT NULL | | CHECK in (`passed`, `failed`) |\n| `evaluated_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | `now()` | |\n\nIndex on `evaluated_at` serves the latest-evaluation lookup.\n\n**`residency.scan_runs`**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | uuid | NOT NULL | generated | PRIMARY KEY |\n| `trigger` | text | NOT NULL | | CHECK in (`scheduled`, `manual`) |\n| `status` | text | NOT NULL | `'running'` | CHECK in (`running`, `completed`, `failed`) |\n| `started_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | `now()` | |\n| `completed_at` | timestamptz | NULL | | |\n| `drift_detected` | boolean | NULL | | NULL until completed |\n| `alert_status` | text | NULL | | CHECK in (`not_required`, `sent`, `failed`) |\n| `failure_reason` | text | NULL | | |\n\nIndex on `started_at` serves the latest-scan lookup.\n\n**`residency.scan_findings`**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | uuid | NOT NULL | generated | PRIMARY KEY |\n| `scan_run_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | | FK `scan_runs(id)` ON DELETE RESTRICT |\n| `resource_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | | FK `resources(id)` ON DELETE RESTRICT |\n| `observed_region` | text | NULL | | |\n| `finding_type` | text | NOT NULL | | CHECK in (`non_eu`, `unreadable`, `unrecognized_type`) |\n\nIndex on `scan_run_id`.\n\nAll `ON DELETE RESTRICT` choices reflect that evidence rows are never deleted by this module; no input defines deletion (`GAP-BE-276`). Encryption at rest is not stated by any input and no claim is made (`GAP-BE-006` of the project spec).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## State machine\n\nEntities with a status column or a decided outcome:\n\n**Resource `verification_status`**\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| (new) | `unverified` | Inventory record created, or resource type not recognised by the scanner | none (AC-001-04-02) |\n| `unverified`, `non_compliant`, `verified` | `verified` | Verification reads a region | region is in `approved_regions` with `active = true` |\n| `unverified`, `verified` | `non_compliant` | Verification reads a region | region is not an active approved region |\n| `verified`, `non_compliant` | `unverified` | Verification cannot read the region | `region` is NULL |\n\nNo state is terminal. Status is re-derived on each verification. An illegal transition cannot occur, because the target is a function of the observed region.\n\n**Provisioning request `decision`**\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| (new) | `accepted` | Request evaluated | requested region is an active approved region (AC-001-01-01) |\n| (new) | `rejected` | Request evaluated | requested region is not approved, or the approved list is empty (AC-001-01-02, `ASM-BE-270`) |\n\nBoth outcomes are terminal. There is no transition between them.\n\n**Backup validation `valid`.** Decided once at evaluation and terminal. `valid = true` only if the primary and every replica region is an active approved region. Otherwise `false`, within the 30-second deadline (AC-001-02-02, `ASM-BE-273`).\n\n**CI gate check `verdict`.** Decided once and terminal. `pass` only if every target region is read and approved. Any non-EU, missing or unreadable region gives `fail` (AC-001-03-01 to 03).\n\n**Launch gate** (`launch_gate_evaluations.status`, derived per `ASM-BE-272`)\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| (none) | `passed` | Evaluation on a freshly generated report | `percent_compliant` = 100 and no `unverified` item (AC-001-05-01) |\n| (none) | `failed` | Evaluation on a freshly generated report | otherwise (AC-001-05-02) |\n\nEach evaluation row is terminal, but the gate is not: the next evaluation can produce a different status. \"Blocked until resolved\" is met by the next evaluation after the resource is fixed.\n\n**Scan run `status`**\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `running` | `completed` | All resources read and findings stored | none |\n| `running` | `failed` | Scan could not complete | `failure_reason` recorded |\n\n`completed` and `failed` are terminal. On `completed`, `drift_detected` is set, and `alert_status` is set to `not_required` when there are no findings (AC-001-06-01) or to `sent` or `failed` otherwise. A scan failure does not mean \"no drift\": it is surfaced as failed, never as a pass (`INV-BE-257`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.requirements | status: BLOCKED -->\n## Requirements\n\n**BLOCKED.** the run did not produce this section for module `residency`. Nothing has been written in its place.\n\nThis section is required by the surface's template and was not produced. It is emitted blocked rather than omitted: a missing section is invisible, a blocked one is a work item.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\nNo HTTP operation is defined.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.invariants | status: DERIVED -->\n## Invariants\n\n**INV-BE-251** A resource is `verified` only if its `region` is non-NULL and an active approved region. No other state counts toward `verified_count`. (BR-001, AC-001-04-02.)\n\n**INV-BE-252** A `rejected` provisioning request has `resource_id` NULL; no resource row exists because of it. Enforced by CHECK. (AC-001-01-02.)\n\n**INV-BE-253** A CI gate check has verdict `pass` only if every target region is read and approved. A missing, unreadable or non-EU region gives `fail`. (AC-001-03-01 to 03.)\n\n**INV-BE-254** A backup validation that is not `valid` records `violating_regions` and no artefact reference. This module never causes a backup write. (AC-001-02-02.)\n\n**INV-BE-255** For every report, `percent_compliant` = `verified_count` / `total_resources` \u00d7 100, `unverified` resources are in the denominator, and the item count equals `total_resources`. (AC-001-04-01, `ASM-BE-271`.)\n\n**INV-BE-256** A launch gate evaluation is `passed` only if its report has `percent_compliant` = 100 and no `unverified` item. The report is generated at evaluation time. (BR-003, NFR-004, `ASM-BE-272`.)\n\n**INV-BE-257** A scan that did not reach `completed` never reports `drift_detected = false`. A `failed` scan is surfaced, not treated as a pass. (BR-004.)\n\n**INV-BE-258** A `completed` scan with at least one finding has `alert_status` of `sent` or `failed`, never `not_required`. (AC-001-06-02.)\n\n**INV-BE-259** Decision and evidence rows (`provisioning_requests`, `backup_validations`, `ci_gate_checks`, reports, evaluations, completed scans and findings) are append-only and never updated after completion. Resource rows are updated only through verification.\n\n**INV-BE-260** With no active approved region, no outcome is accept, valid, `pass` or `verified` (BE-RESIDENCY-FR-014).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.authorization | status: BLOCKED -->\n## Authorization\n\n**BLOCKED.** The role-by-operation matrix and any ownership predicate cannot be written without inventing an authorisation rule. `backend.auth-model` is itself `BLOCKED`, and no input says how callers of these operations are authenticated or which role may invoke which operation (`GAP-BE-271`).\n\nThe stories name these intended users, which is not a permission matrix:\n\n- Platform/DevOps Engineer: provisioning, backups, drift alerts (US-001-01, 02, 06).\n- Release/CI Pipeline Owner: the CI check (US-001-03).\n- Compliance/Security Auditor: the compliance report (US-001-04).\n- Engineering Lead: launch gate sign-off (US-001-05, BR-003).\n\nThe data is not user-scoped and carries no owner column, so no ownership predicate applies.\n\n**What would unblock it.** A decision on how the pipeline and the named humans authenticate, and which roles may invoke each of the seven operations. Close `GAP-BE-271` and the project-level auth decisions.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Jobs\n\n| Job | Trigger | Behaviour | Idempotency key | Retry |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Drift scan | Schedule, plus manual trigger | Reads every inventory resource, records findings, sets `drift_detected`, raises an alert when findings exist (`ASM-BE-274`) | none; each run is a new `scan_runs` row | A `failed` run is not retried silently; the next scheduled run proceeds |\n| Report refresh | Each launch gate evaluation and each report request | Generates a fresh `compliance_reports` row so the report is accurate at each launch gate check (NFR-004) | none; each generation is a new row | none |\n| CI gate check | Each deployment | Evaluates `target_regions` | `deployment_id`; a repeat creates a new row (`ASM-BE-277`) | The pipeline may re-run; the verdict is recomputed |\n| Backup config validation | Before each backup run | Evaluates the configuration within 30 seconds | none | A timeout is `valid = false` (`ASM-BE-273`) |\n\n**Scan cadence** is not stated: \"periodic\" only (AC-001-06-01). It is a configuration value with a proposed default of 15 minutes (`ASM-BE-278`, `Q-BE-255`). The alert deadline is 15 minutes from detection (NFR-003), and detection happens when a scan observes the resource.\n\n**Not specified by any input:** alert delivery retries (`GAP-BE-278`), and retention sweeps for residency records (`GAP-BE-276`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Test plan\n\n**Unit**\n- Region comparison: approved, not approved, NULL, empty list (BE-RESIDENCY-FR-014).\n- Verification status derivation for a resource.\n- Percent computation with `unverified` in the denominator and exact two-decimal rounding.\n- Launch gate derivation at 100%, below 100%, and with an `unverified` item.\n- Backup deadline: a validation exceeding 30 seconds returns invalid.\n\n**Integration** (PostgreSQL 16)\n- Constraint tests: CHECK on `rejected` with `resource_id`, `valid` against `violating_regions`, `verdict` against `violations`.\n- Provisioning reject leaves no `resources` row.\n- Drift scan with a seeded non-EU resource stores a finding and sets `alert_status`.\n- Alert delivery failure sets `alert_status = failed` and keeps the finding.\n- Report snapshot has one item per resource at generation time.\n- Append-only: completed decision rows cannot be updated.\n\n**Contract** (method and path until ids are minted)\n- Each of the seven operations returns only its documented success and error statuses, taken from the published contract.\n- The CI gate check fails for each of: a non-EU region, a missing region, an unreadable region.\n- No contract test relies on an approved region list that is not supplied (`GAP-BE-272`).\n\nNamed tests are in `BE-RESIDENCY-FR-001` to `BE-RESIDENCY-FR-014`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.gaps | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Gaps\n\n**Gaps**\n\n| ID | Gap | Degrades | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-270` | No operation records were supplied to this module, so no `API-` ids exist. Operations are named by method and path from the prior approved spec. | `scope:`, `api-contract`, API lines in requirements | Operation records from DevAgent |\n| `GAP-BE-271` | Which roles may invoke which residency operation, and how pipelines authenticate, is not stated. | `authorization` (BLOCKED) | Auth-model decision and a role matrix for these operations |\n| `GAP-BE-272` | The approved EU region list is not supplied. | `data-model`, FR-001 to FR-007, FR-014 | A requirement or platform input listing the approved regions |\n| `GAP-BE-273` | Which resource types make up the inventory, and how a region is read for each, is not stated. | `data-model` (`resources`), FR-008, FR-009 | A platform input naming resource types and region sources |\n| `GAP-BE-274` | The \"periodic\" scan cadence is not stated. | `jobs` | A requirement stating the interval |\n| `GAP-BE-275` | The alert channel and recipient mechanism for the DevOps team are not stated. | FR-013 | An alerting or on-call requirement |\n| `GAP-BE-276` | Retention and deletion of residency evidence (reports, scans, findings, validations) are not stated. See also `GAP-BE-006`. | `data-model` | A data-retention requirement |\n| `GAP-BE-277` | Whether the Engineering Lead's sign-off (BR-003) is a recorded API action or an outside process is not stated. This module derives the gate only. | FR-010, FR-011 | A decision on the sign-off mechanism |\n| `GAP-BE-278` | Alert delivery retry and escalation behaviour are not stated. | FR-013, `jobs` | An alerting requirement |\n\n**Contradiction**\n\n- `CON-BE-254` (non-critical). AC-001-06-02 says an alert is sent \"immediately\". NFR-003 says the alert is fired \"within 15 minutes of drift detection\". Reading used: send on scan completion, with 15 minutes as the upper bound (`ASM-BE-276`). Choosing \"immediately\" as a hard requirement would add a latency target that no other input supports.\n\n**Questions** (all degradable; unattended defaults apply)\n\n- `Q-BE-254` (degradable). What are the approved EU regions? Default: none supplied, so every check fails closed (`ASM-BE-270`).\n- `Q-BE-255` (degradable). What is the drift scan interval? Default: 15 minutes (`ASM-BE-278`).\n- `Q-BE-256` (degradable). How is the DevOps team alerted? Default: the alert is recorded in `alert_status` and logged as an event; transport is left to infrastructure.\n\n**Assumptions**\n\n| ID | Assumption |\n|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-267` | The seven operations are those listed in the prior approved BACKEND-SPEC attached to `feedback.md` (CR-131). Their ids are not reused because this run did not mint them. |\n| `ASM-BE-268` | Residency tables live in a `residency` schema of the same PostgreSQL 16 database, in an EU region. `platform.md` names PostgreSQL 16 only. |\n| `ASM-BE-269` | Ids are UUIDs and timestamps are `timestamptz` in UTC. No input fixes either. |\n| `ASM-BE-270` | A region is EU iff it exactly matches an active row of `approved_regions`. An empty list fails closed. |\n| `ASM-BE-271` | `unverified` resources count in the denominator and not as compliant. `percent_compliant` is `numeric(5,2)`. AC-001-04-02 only requires them to be flagged. |\n| `ASM-BE-272` | The launch gate is derived from a report generated at evaluation time, because NFR-004 requires the report to be refreshed at each gate check. |\n| `ASM-BE-273` | The 30-second backup deadline runs from receipt of the validation request to the failure result. A timeout is treated as invalid, not as a pass. |\n| `ASM-BE-274` | Each completed scan with one or more findings raises exactly one alert, with no deduplication across scans. |\n| `ASM-BE-275` | This module records provisioning decisions only. The real \"no resource is created\" is enforced by infrastructure-as-code region constraints (BR-001). |\n| `ASM-BE-276` | \"Immediately\" (AC-001-06-02) means at scan completion. NFR-003's 15 minutes bounds the time from detection to the alert. |\n| `ASM-BE-277` | A repeated CI check on the same deployment identifier creates a new row. |\n| `ASM-BE-278` | The proposed default scan interval is 15 minutes. It is unsourced and is the value `Q-BE-255` asks about. |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-001-01", "US-001-02", "US-001-03", "US-001-04", "US-001-05", "US-001-06"]}, {"id": "backend/contracts/openapi.yaml", "type": "openapi", "path": "backend/contracts/openapi.yaml", "content": "openapi: 3.1.0\ninfo:\n  title: Backend API\n  version: 1.0.0\n  description: Operations the backend module specifications define, rendered from\n    their structured operation records. None was inferred.\npaths:\n  /auth/request-code:\n    post:\n      operationId: API-AUTH-015365\n      summary: Request a 6-digit one-time login code, emailed to the supplied address.\n        Limited to 3 requests per rolling 10 minutes per email; not refused during\n        an account lock (BR-032).\n      description: Defined by module `auth` in `modules/auth-SPEC.md`.\n      requestBody:\n        required: true\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              additionalProperties: false\n              required:\n              - email\n              properties:\n                email:\n                  type: string\n                  description: Email address to which the one-time login code is sent.\n                    PII; always masked in examples and logs.\n      responses:\n        '202':\n          description: Request a 6-digit one-time login code, emailed to the supplied\n            address. Limited to 3 requests per rolling 10 minutes per email; not refused\n            during an account lock (BR-032).\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                type: object\n                additionalProperties: false\n                required: []\n                properties:\n                  message:\n                    type: string\n                    description: Human-readable confirmation that a code was issued\n                      and emailed. Never contains the code or an unmasked email address.\n        '400':\n          description: See `modules/auth-SPEC.md`, API-AUTH-015365.\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                $ref: '#/components/schemas/AuthError'\n        '429':\n          description: See `modules/auth-SPEC.md`, API-AUTH-015365.\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                $ref: '#/components/schemas/AuthError'\n        '500':\n          description: See `modules/auth-SPEC.md`, API-AUTH-015365.\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                $ref: '#/components/schemas/AuthError'\n      x-devagent-source:\n        module: auth\n        document: modules/auth-SPEC.md\n        id: API-AUTH-015365\n  /auth/verify-code:\n    post:\n      operationId: API-AUTH-592570\n      summary: 'Verify a one-time login code. Codes expire 10 minutes after issue\n        and are single use. Five wrong codes lock verification for 15 minutes. Precedence:\n        lock, then used, then expired, then invalid.'\n      description: Defined by module `auth` in `modules/auth-SPEC.md`.\n      requestBody:\n        required: true\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              additionalProperties: false\n              required:\n              - email\n              - code\n              properties:\n                email:\n                  type: string\n                  description: Email address the code was issued to. PII; always masked\n                    in examples and logs.\n                code:\n                  type: string\n                  description: The 6-digit numeric one-time code received by email.\n      responses:\n        '200':\n          description: 'Verify a one-time login code. Codes expire 10 minutes after\n            issue and are single use. Five wrong codes lock verification for 15 minutes.\n            Precedence: lock, then used, then expired, then invalid.'\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                type: object\n                additionalProperties: false\n                required: []\n                properties:\n                  verified:\n                    type: boolean\n                    description: True when the code was accepted and consumed. What\n                      credential a successful verify issues (type, lifetime, transport)\n                      is undecided and blocked on Q-BE-003, so no credential field\n                      is defined.\n        '400':\n          description: See `modules/auth-SPEC.md`, API-AUTH-592570.\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                $ref: '#/components/schemas/AuthError'\n        '401':\n          description: See `modules/auth-SPEC.md`, API-AUTH-592570.\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n API-AUTH-592570.\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                $ref: '#/components/schemas/AuthError'\n      x-devagent-source:\n        module: auth\n optional tags\n        (US-002-01, US-006-02).\n      description: Defined by module `notes` in `modules/notes-SPEC.md`.\n      requestBody:\n        required: true\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              additionalProperties: false\n              required:\n              - title\n              - body\n              properties:\n                title:\n                  type: string\n                  description: Note title; required (BR-005).\n                body:\n                  type: string\n                  description: Note body; required (BR-005).\n                pinned:\n                  type: boolean\n                  description: Pinned flag; defaults to false when omitted (US-002-01,\n                    BR-018).\n                tags:\n                  type: array\n                  description: Initial tag names to attach to the note; duplicates\n                    create no duplicate error\n      properties:\n        error:\n          type: object\n          description: 'Error envelope per backend.error-model (ASM-BE-001): contains\n            code (stable machine-readable registry code, e.g. NOT_ACCEPTABLE or INTERNAL_ERROR),\n            message (human-readable reason) and details (array).'\n"}, {"id": "backend/HANDOFFS.md", "type": "handoffs", "path": "backend/HANDOFFS.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.handoffs | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Needs this lane raised\n\n| Need | Kind | What is needed | Why | Blocking | Waiting sections |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `HANDOFF-NOTESAPI-067561` | `business_rule` | Provide a clean statement of BR-030: who may request, verify or receive a login code (any email address, or only a known set). | The auth model cannot define actors or roles without it (Q-BE-001). | yes | `backend.auth-model`, `backend.api-surface` |\n| `HANDOFF-NOTESAPI-501376` | `business_rule` | Decide whether notes are owned per user or shared in the team workspace (BR-031, HANDOFF-NOTESAPI-981573), and give the access rule for each note operation. | The access predicate, the ownership columns (migration 007) and the role matrix depend on it (Q-BE-002). | yes | `backend.auth-model`, `backend.domain-model`, `backend.migration-plan` |\n| `HANDOFF-NOTESAPI-345681` | `business_rule` | Decide what a successful code verification issues (credential type, lifetime, transport, refresh and logout) and whether the notes and search endpoints require it. | The verify response, 401/403 applicability on other endpoints and the web_app session handling depend on it (Q-BE-003). | yes | `backend.auth-model`, `backend.api-surface`, `backend.error-model` |\n| `HANDOFF-NOTESAPI-418421` | `story` | Supply a story or decision for list operations: active notes (pinned first), archived-only notes, and distinct tags. Search cannot substitute because it needs a keyword or tag. | US-006-01, US-006-05 and the tag filter have no backend data source (GAP-BE-005). | no | `backend.api-surface`, `backend.coverage` |\n| `HANDOFF-NOTESAPI-558747` | `business_rule` | Specify the remaining lock time returned on a 423, Retry-After on 429 and 503, and the rejection status for WebSocket or SSE attempts. | The error model cannot give these values without inventing them (GAP-BE-011). | no | `backend.error-model`, `backend.api-surface` |\n| `HANDOFF-NOTESAPI-590636` | `business_rule` | Provide tag rules: case, allowed characters, length and maximum tags per note. | The tag validation in create and add-tag is open (GAP-BE-010). | no | `backend.domain-model`, `backend.api-surface` |\n\n## Needs this lane cannot answer\n\n| Need | From lane | Asked | Why not |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `HANDOFF-WEBAPP-500592` | `web_app` | Decide and define the session model: what a successful code verification issues (credential type, lifetime, transport), refresh and logout operations, and whether notes and search endpoints require a session (Q-BE-003). | The session model is undecided. It is blocking question Q-BE-003 and needs a human decision, so the backend spec defines no credential, refresh or logout. |\n| `HANDOFF-WEBAPP-732181` | `web_app` | Add operations to list active notes and to list archived-only notes, since search requires q or a tag and include_archived mixes both states. | The backend spec has no operation that lists active or archived-only notes (GAP-BE-005). Adding one is a scope decision that needs a story or decision from the product side. |\n| `HANDOFF-WEBAPP-263975` | `web_app` | Add an operation that lists distinct tags, usable for the tag filter options and the tags list screen. | The backend spec has no operation that lists distinct tags. No input defines one, and tag rules are also open (GAP-BE-010, GAP-BE-005), so a story or decision is needed first. |\n| `HANDOFF-WEBAPP-452996` | `web_app` | Specify the remaining lock time, the re-enable rule, precedence among simultaneous verify refusals, whether a new code can be requested while locked, and the final error-code registry. | Only partly answerable. Request-code is allowed during a lock (BR-032), and verify precedence is proposed in ASM-BE-007. The remaining lock time, the re-enable rule and the final error-code registry are not defined by any input (GAP-BE-011, ASM-BE-001). |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.handoffs -->\n"}], "notices": []}
